### [1] [First-Principles Determination of the QCD Contribution to the Axion-Photon Coupling Using Domain-Wall Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22698)
*Tian Lin,Hao-Yang Cheng,Xu Feng,Lu-Chang Jin,Chuan Liu,Qi-Yuan Luo*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点QCD方法，首次独立确定了QCD动力学对轴子-光子耦合常数C_QCD的贡献，得到C_QCD = 1.965(35)，并指出与NLO ChPT结果的表观矛盾源于系统性处理差异，二者可自洽统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决NLO手征微扰论（ChPT）在计算轴子-光子耦合的QCD贡献C_QCD时出现的高达8%的框架间分歧，亟需基于第一性原理的精确格点QCD基准结果。

Method: 采用互补于背景场法的格点方法：计算赝标介子→双光子三点半关联函数，结合反常Ward恒等式将C_QCD分解为精确反常项与轻夸克质量压低的修正项；使用具有优异手征对称性的域壁费米子，并在两个近物理N_f=2+1组态系综上完成连续极限外推。

Result: 获得C_QCD^IS = 1.619(30)（同位旋对称部分）、C_QCD^IB = 0.347(22)（同位旋破缺部分），总和C_QCD = 1.965(35)；确认该结果与经系统性修正后的NLO ChPT预言一致。

Conclusion: 本工作提供了轴子-光子耦合中QCD贡献的首个第一性原理基准值，定量检验并统一了不同ChPT框架，为轴子实验搜寻与强CP问题检验奠定关键理论基础。

Abstract: The axion-photon coupling, crucial for experimental axion searches and tests of the strong CP solution, receives a substantial model-independent contribution $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}$ from QCD dynamics. Next-to-leading-order chiral perturbation theory (NLO ChPT) in different frameworks has yielded puzzling discrepancies of up to $8\%$, motivating precise first-principles calculations. We present an independent lattice QCD determination using a method complementary to the recent background-field calculation. Computing pseudoscalar-to-two-photon three-point functions and exploiting anomalous Ward identities, we separate $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}$ into an exact anomaly contribution and a light-quark-mass-suppressed correction. The latter is computed using domain-wall fermions, whose excellent chiral symmetry strongly suppresses discretization effects. Working on two near-physical $N_f=2+1$ ensembles with continuum extrapolation, we obtain $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}^{\rm IS}=1.619(30)$ (isospin-symmetric), $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}^{\rm IB}=0.347(22)$ (isospin-breaking), and $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}=1.965(35)$ in total. While direct comparisons with published NLO ChPT predictions reveal apparent tensions, we identify their sources and show that the ChPT results can be reconciled with our lattice determination. Our result provides a first-principles benchmark for the QCD contribution to the axion-photon coupling and a quantitative test of competing ChPT descriptions.

</details>


### [2] [Flowed quark field renormalization in lattice QCD: A Ward-identity approach and its validation using quark bilinears](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23379)
*Mei-Qi Gao,Long-cheng Gui,Jun Hua,Jian Liang,Jun Shi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Ward恒等式的非微扰方法，用于确定流化夸克场的重整化因子 $Z_χ$，避免了传统‘ringed’方法的计算困难；该方法利用矢量流归一化与流化/未流化介子两点函数比值，在五组2+1味clover费米子组态上进行了计算，并在赝标量、标量、轴矢量和张量通道中验证了其一致性；结果与RI/MOM和RI/SMOM方法一致，且格点间距依赖性随格点变细而减小，证明该方案稳健可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统ringed prescription在计算流化夸克场重整化因子 $Z_χ$ 时的高计算成本与数值不稳定性问题，建立一种严格满足对称性（特别是矢量流守恒）的非微扰方案。

Method: 基于矢量流Ward恒等式构造无环（ringless）的流化夸克场重整化因子 $\mathring{Z}_χ^{V}(t_f,a)$，定义为流化与未流化介子两点函数之比并结合矢量流归一化条件；在五组不同格距的2+1味Wilson-clover费米子组态上实现，采用多通道（PS, S, AV, T）交叉检验；辅以连续极限与零流时间外推。

Result: 成功获得 $\mathring{Z}_χ^{V}(t_f,a)$ 在五组clover组态上的数值结果；各通道间重整化矩阵元高度一致；与RI/MOM及RI/SMOM基准结果在误差范围内吻合；有限格距修正随 $a \to 0$ 显著压低，体现方法的O(a)-改善特性。

Conclusion: 所提Ward恒等式驱动的ringless方案是流化费米子算符非微扰重整化的可靠新范式，兼具理论自洽性、数值稳健性与通道普适性，为后续高精度格点QCD计算（如强子结构、味物理）提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We present a non-perturbative Ward-identity prescription for determining the flowed quark field renormalization factor $Z_χ$, avoiding the computational difficulties of the conventional ringed prescription. The method is based on vector-current normalization and ratios of flowed and unflowed meson two-point functions. We determine the resulting $\mathring{Z}_χ^{V}(t_f,a)$ on five $2+1$-flavor clover ensembles and validate it in the pseudoscalar, scalar, axial-vector, and tensor channels. Renormalized matrix elements obtained through sequential continuum and zero-flow-time extrapolations agree with independent RI/MOM and RI/SMOM determinations. The finite-lattice-spacing bilinear renormalization factors show differences that decrease toward finer lattices, reflecting the different discretization effects of the renormalization methods. The cross-channel agreement demonstrates the viability of the proposed prescription; together, the method and its systematic validation establish a robust foundation for the non-perturbative renormalization of flowed fermionic operators in future lattice calculations.

</details>

*V. Martínez-Fernández,B. Pire,P. Sznajder,J. Wagner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了氦-4核上的深度虚康普顿散射（DVCS），包含领头扭度（twist-2）及运动学扭度-3、扭度-4效应，给出了JLab实验相关运动学区域的截面与不对称性数值估计，并首次构建了氦-4核内夸克与胶子的三维层析图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用相干硬末态排斥过程（如DVCS）探测轻核的夸克-胶子结构，实现核子尺度下的三维核结构成像（nuclear tomography）。

Method: 在领头扭度（twist-2）框架下，系统纳入运动学扭度-3和扭度-4修正，并结合JLab实验可及的运动学区域进行数值计算；采用广义部分子分布（GPDs）描述核层次的强子结构，考虑核束缚与Fermi运动效应。

Result: 给出了4He DVCS微分截面与单自旋不对称（SSA）的定量预测；指出在JLab能量下，NLO QCD辐射修正及运动学扭度-3/4项对可观测量有显著贡献；首次重构出4He核内夸克与胶子的空间-动量联合分布（即三维核GPD层析图）。

Conclusion: 氦-4 DVCS是实现核子尺度三维强子结构成像的有效探针；扭度-3/4与NLO效应不可忽略，必须纳入理论分析以准确提取核GPD信息；该工作为未来EIC上更重核的胶子层析研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Coherent hard exclusive reactions on light nuclei offer access to their quark and gluon structure and provide a framework for three-dimensional nuclear tomography. We analyze deeply virtual Compton scattering on a helium-4 target, including leading-twist contributions as well as kinematical twist-3 and twist-4 effects. We present numerical estimates of cross sections and asymmetries for kinematic regimes relevant to JLab experiments. We emphasize the importance of next to leading order (NLO) and kinematical twist 3 and 4 contributions to interpret data in the JLab kinematics. We deduce a first tomographic image of quarks and gluons in the helium-4 nucleus.

</details>


### [4] [Resonant Gravitational Production of Chiral Dark Photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22423)
*Leah Jenks,Marc Kamionkowski,Edward W. Kolb*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在宇宙暴胀时期，具有手性、宇称破缺耦合的矢量场（暗光子）通过引力粒子产生机制被共振放大现象。分析了两种产生机制：由耦合与质量比决定的快失稳（tachyonic instability）和参量共振（parametric resonance），指出横向模态可主导晚期丰度，从而拓宽暗光子暗物质可行参数空间；快失稳情形下产生单螺旋度主导的**手性暗光子**，带来可观测的宇称破缺效应；并揭示产额谱携带再加热历史信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释暗光子作为暗物质候选者的产生机制，尤其关注宇称破缺耦合诱导的非平凡动力学（如手性放大、螺旋度选择），以及其对早期宇宙演化（如暴胀后 reheating）的敏感性。

Method: 基于暴胀背景时空中的弯曲时空量子场论，求解含宇称奇耦合项 g φ F_μν \tilde{F}^{μν} 的重矢量场运动方程；区分 tachyonic 与 parametric resonance 参数区域；计算各螺旋度模的 Bogoliubov 系数与能谱；结合再加热过程建模，分析数密度谱对再加热温度与持续时间的依赖。

Result: 1) 横向模态因手性耦合被显著增强，可超越纵向模态成为主导成分；2) tachyonic 区域中单一螺旋度（±1）被指数放大，生成高纯度手性暗光子；3) 暗光子暗物质可行参数空间扩展至更小质量与更弱耦合区域；4) 数密度谱的形状（如峰值位置与截断尺度）编码再加热温度 T_reh 与状态方程 w_reh。

Conclusion: 宇称奇耦合使暗光子产生机制具备内在手征性与再加热敏感性，不仅提供新暗物质产生通道，且其残余谱可作为探测暴胀后物理的‘化石探针’。

Abstract: Vector fields with a chiral, parity-odd coupling to a pseudoscalar field are known to undergo resonant amplification. In this work, we study this phenomenon in the context of cosmological gravitational particle production during early-universe inflation. We consider a massive vector (dark photon) field with a parity-odd coupling to the inflaton. There are two regimes of production, delineated by the relative size of the coupling and the dark photon mass, arising from a tachyonic instability, and a parametric resonance, respectively. As a result of the coupling, the transverse modes, subdominant in both the minimally and nonminimally coupled theories, can dominate the total late-time abundance. The viable parameter space for dark-photon dark matter is thus widened. Furthermore, in the tachyonic case, the production is dominated by a single helicity mode, leading to a population of chiral dark photons that can lead to potential parity-violating observational signatures. We further explore the dependence of the production on the reheating history, and find that information about reheating is encoded in the number density spectrum.

</details>


### [5] [Tackling the noisy truncated moment problem with Gaussian Processes: An application to parton distribution functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23196)
*Rohith Karur*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了带噪声的截断Hausdorff矩问题，旨在从矩数据中重建部分子分布函数（PDFs），提出了一种基于高斯过程（GP）的贝叶斯方法，并引入了校正后验均值偏差与自适应x相关长度核等新工具，在多类PDF数据集上验证了其鲁棒性，适用于格点QCD第一性原理计算的PDF重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限、含噪声的矩数据中稳健重建物理上合理的PDFs，尤其面向格点QCD输出的低阶矩，解决传统矩反演方法在非光滑性、欠定性和噪声敏感性方面的根本局限。

Method: 采用以高斯过程为核心的贝叶斯回归框架：系统分析不同PDF函数类（如Hölder连续、解析类）下矩数与可重构性的关系；设计具有可学习x方向相关长度的协方差核；推导解析后验分布；结合MCMC进行超参数采样；并提出后验均值偏差校正技术。

Result: 在5个涵盖价夸克、胶子和海夸克的π介子与核子PDF数据集上，仅用中等数量（~10–20阶）的含噪矩即实现高保真PDF重建；新提出的偏差校正与自适应核显著改善后验不确定性量化与边缘行为拟合精度；框架对矩截断误差与测量噪声具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于GP的贝叶斯矩反演是解决噪声截断Hausdorff矩问题的有效范式，为从格点QCD矩数据第一性原理提取PDFs提供了严格、灵活且实用的数学物理工具。

Abstract: In this work, we study the noisy truncated Hausdorff moment problem in the context of recovering parton distribution functions (PDFs) from their moments. We start by reviewing basics of the Hausdorff moment problem and analyze how the relation between the amount of moment data provided and the ability for $f(x)$ to be reconstructed varies across different classes of parton distribution functions; we then turn to using Bayesian methods centered around Gaussian Processes (GPs) to solve the moment problems. Standard aspects of Bayesian regression such as covariance kernel choice, analytical characterization of the posterior, hyperparameter sampling with Monte Carlo methods, and validation metrics are detailed. In addition to this, powerful novel tools, such as a method to correct for mean bias in posterior distributions, and a simple class of covariance kernels whose $x$ correlation length is learned during sampling are introduced.
  Finally, we test our framework on 5 datasets from valence, gluon, and sea quark phenomenological pion and nucleon PDF datasets, selected to reflect diversity in PDF behavior. We find that our GP framework robustly reconstructs PDFs given a moderate number of moments. Additionally, the novel tools developed in this work resolve persistent issues that would be difficult to address via conventional means. The presented framework can be readily applied to moments obtained via lattice Quantum Chromodynamics (LQCD) to reconstruct PDFs from theoretical first principles.

</details>


### [6] [Gravitational Waves from Long Strings and Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22430)
*Christos Litos,Sarunas Verner,Wei Xue,Fengwei Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了全局宇宙弦网络在标度不变态下产生的引力波谱，将其分为红外和紫外两部分；红外部分通过推广的未连接弦段模型解析推导，紫外部分则通过数据驱动方法连接格点模拟结果；预测谱在红外呈k或k^3增长，在紫外趋于平缓带对数倾斜；转折频率由轴子质量决定，并据此给出对Peccei-Quinn对称破缺能标f_a与轴子质量m_a的当前约束与未来探测前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全局宇宙弦网络作为轴子暗物质候选源所引发的引力波信号，特别是其频谱特征如何依赖于轴子质量与对称破缺能标，从而为轴子参数空间提供新的天体物理探针。

Method: 采用不等时关联函数（unequal-time correlator）方法计算弦应力-能量张量涨落，在未连接弦段模型（unconnected segment model）框架下解析处理红外区；首次将该模型推广至弦环贡献；紫外区则通过数据驱动方式桥接格点模拟所得瞬时辐射功率谱。

Result: 预测引力波能量密度谱Ω_GW(k)在红外区随波数k线性（k）或立方（k^3）增长，取决于主导贡献（长弦或环）；紫外区呈现平台状，具弱对数斜率；谱转折频率由轴子质量m_a决定；由此在(f_a, m_a)平面上给出LIGO/Virgo、PTA及未来探测器（如LISA、BBO）的约束与灵敏度边界。

Conclusion: 全局宇宙弦网络产生的引力波谱具有清晰可测的频谱结构，其红外-紫外转折特征直接编码轴子质量信息，为联合引力波观测与轴子物理建模提供了关键理论桥梁，并显著拓展了轴子参数空间的可观测窗口。

Abstract: We compute the gravitational wave spectrum produced by a global cosmic string network in the scaling regime. The spectrum naturally divides at the string correlation length into infrared and ultraviolet parts. In the infrared, we derive the spectrum analytically from the unequal-time correlator of the string stress-energy tensor within the unconnected segment model, which we generalize for the first time to string loops. The loop contribution can be comparable to that of long strings, depending on the loop evolution parameters. In the ultraviolet, where structure below the correlation length dominates the source, we develop a data-driven method that connects this correlator formalism to the instantaneous radiation power spectrum measured in existing lattice simulations. The predicted spectrum rises with frequency as $Ω_{\rm GW}\propto k$ and $k^{3}$ in the infrared, and flattens into a plateau with a mild logarithmic tilt in the ultraviolet. The turnover frequency between the two is set by the mass of the Goldstone boson, the axion. Confronting the predicted spectrum with current data and projected sensitivities, we map out current constraints and future probes in the plane of the symmetry-breaking scale~$f_a$ and the axion mass~$m_a$.

</details>


### [7] [Spectroscopic parameters of $B_c$ meson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24536)
*Sinem Küçükyılmaz,Halil Mutuk*

Main category: hep-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确理解Bc介子作为唯一含两个不同重味夸克（b和c）的束缚态，对检验QCD非微扰机制、自旋-轨道耦合及夸克模型适用性具有独特价值；尤其其激发态尚未充分实验观测，理论预测亟需关键可测物理量以指导实验。

Method: 采用非相对论夸克模型（NRQM），结合一阶QCD修正计算赝标量与矢量衰变常数；利用偶极（E1）与磁偶极（M1）跃迁算符计算辐射宽度；通过求解薛定谔方程获得S/P/D波径向与轨道激发态能谱；构造并拟合径向Regge轨迹；细致分析OGE与禁闭势对自旋-轨道相互作用的竞争效应。

Result: 1) 基态及第一径向激发态质量与实验一致；2) 衰变常数与相对论模型相容，给出Bc→τντ分支比预测；3) E1跃迁主导D→P→S级联路径，为探测未观测D波态提供方案；4) P/D波多重态呈现反常精细结构序（inverted fine-structure），源于OGE与禁闭势对自旋-轨道项的竞争；5) M1宽度因超精细劈裂过小而严重压低，凸显Bc*质量直接测量的必要性；6) 各波族径向Regge轨迹近似线性，斜率随轨道角动量ℓ增大而减小。

Conclusion: Bc系统是检验QCD自旋依赖相互作用的理想平台；理论预测明确指向两项关键实验检验：Bc*介子质量的精确测定，以及最低P波多重态（1P, 2P）精细结构的能量序（即1P0/1P1/1P2的相对位置），二者将严格约束OGE与禁闭势在自旋-轨道耦合中的相对权重。

Abstract: We investigate the spectroscopic and decay properties of the $B_c$ meson within a nonrelativistic quark model. We calculate the mass spectrum of radially and orbitally excited states in the $S$-, $P$-, and $D$-wave sectors, the pseudoscalar and vector decay constants with one-loop QCD corrections, the E1 and M1 radiative widths, and radial Regge trajectories. The ground state and its first radial excitation agree well with experiment. The predicted decay constants are in line with relativistic model estimates and yield a corresponding prediction for the leptonic decay $B_c\toτν_τ$. The E1 transition pattern is governed primarily by radial-wave-function overlaps, and the dominant transitions of the lowest $D$-wave multiplet suggest the $D\to P\to S$ radiative cascade as a promising pathway to the unobserved $D$-wave states. The model predicts an inverted fine-structure ordering in the $P$- and $D$-wave multiplets, reflecting the competition between the one-gluon-exchange (OGE) and confinement contributions to the spin--orbit interaction. The allowed M1 widths are strongly suppressed by the small predicted hyperfine splittings, highlighting the importance of a direct $B_c^*$ mass measurement. The radial Regge trajectories are nearly linear across all considered families, with slopes decreasing as the orbital angular momentum increases. These results identify the $B_c^*$ mass and the fine-structure ordering of the lowest $P$-wave multiplet as the key measurements for testing the spin-dependent dynamics of the $B_c$ system.

</details>


### [8] [Non-Perturbative Modulus Decay and Multi-Component Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22438)
*Gordon Kane,Leia Price,Luis Rufino,Scott Watson,Fred Adams*

The events are soft, so trigger thresholds discard most of them, and the Lorentz boost merges the two decay photons into a single calorimeter deposit. Photons that convert to electron-positron pairs in the silicon tracker resolve the merged pair, since the tracker measures their direction far more finely than the calorimeter granularity. Reconstructing the ALP decay vertex from the conversion tracks removes the prompt backgrounds but not the long-lived $K_L\toγγ$. In Run 3, where the full event is recorded, the merged diphoton invariant mass confines the $K_L$ to a narrow window around its own mass. In the scouting stream, where only trigger-level information survives, the diphoton $p_T$, the conversion tracks, and the hadronic activity in the event take over this role. We project that the parked Run 3 data, already recorded, reach a region of the $(m_a, g_{aγγ})$ plane that no measurement has probed. The impact of high energy physics content (through particle physics coefficients) on the magnitude of the nuclear uncertainties is also explored for several WIMP-nucleus scattering responses. Abstract: We investigate the question of equivalence between the Einstein and Jordan frames for generic scalar-tensor and $f(R)$ gravity theories in the context of minisuperspace quantum cosmology. Abstract: Quasinormal modes of black holes provide a central avenue for confronting general relativity with gravitational-wave observations from the ringdown stage of compact binary coalescence. However, inferring black-hole properties or the background spacetime requires theory-dependent input. In this work, we connect a calibrated eikonal method, which relates quasinormal mode shifts to local metric properties near the light ring, with the underlying rotating black hole metric. Using Bayesian inference, we compare a metric-specific model based on the global Konoplya-Rezzolla-Zhidenko parametrization with a metric-agnostic approach based on local values and radial derivatives of the metric functions at the light ring. For each model, we perform both one-parameter and simultaneous multi-parameter analyses and quantify how their priors map onto the same local quantities. We find that uniform priors on the metric-specific coefficients can induce strongly nonuniform and skewed priors on these local metric quantities. For an injection compatible with general relativity, both models remain consistent with Kerr and yield similar posteriors for the orbital frequency and Lyapunov exponent despite their different induced priors. For injections representing deviations from general relativity, one-parameter analyses can fail to recover the injected local deviations and, in the metric-specific case, can bias the global metric reconstruction. The multi-parameter analyses of both models yield consistent constraints, suggesting sufficient robustness. However, they introduce substantial degeneracies and differences in marginalized parameters, demonstrating practical limitations when considering more flexible models. Finally, we apply the framework to approximate posterior information from the ringdown analysis of GW250114 and find that both models are consistent with the Kerr metric in general relativity.

Abstract: Boson stars are self-gravitating solitons of the Einstein-Klein-Gordon equations for a massive complex scalar field, and arguably the simplest horizonless compact objects arising in General Relativity. Beyond spherical symmetry, their construction requires solving a system of nonlinear elliptic partial differential equations, a difficult task per se and that each group has traditionally addressed with its own numerical implementation. A systematic comparison of different methods applied to the same physical model has so far been lacking. In this work, we carry out such a comparison for the two simplest non-spherical configurations: rotating boson stars and static dipolar boson stars. We employ three independent codes, based respectively on finite differences (FIDISOL/CADSOL), finite elements (FreeFem), and spectral methods (Kadath), and assess their accuracy through different diagnostics. The three solvers agree on the global observables to typically eight significant digits. We further compare the runtime of each code on identical hardware and provide precise benchmark values of the relevant physical quantities, intended to serve as reference data for future numerical studies of boson stars. As a further proof of concept, we also present the use of physics-informed neural networks to construct a rotating Q-ball solution.

Abstract: Recent DESI baryon acoustic oscillation measurements, combined with cosmic microwave background and type-Ia supernova data, suggest that dark energy may evolve with cosmic time. Along a cosmological background, an evolving dark-energy density can be expressed locally as a function of a monotonic curvature invariant, motivating an effective curvature-dependent cosmological term. Moreover, quantum field theory in curved spacetime and approaches to quantum gravity with running gravitational couplings provide complementary motivations for exploring such a term.
  Here we study how the phenomenology of nonsingular rotating black holes embedded in a Universe dominated by dark energy can differ substantially from that of the Kerr and Kerr--de Sitter geometries. The problem is directly formulated in phase space using a null geodesic super-Hamiltonian and a Mino-type parameter. Consequently the physical Dysthe models cannot support an inverse-scattering hierarchy with infinitely many linearly independent regular $C^3$ quadratic leading symbols. Method: 采用多尺度数值实验：（1）边界通道位置与路由分离；（2）保持全部透射/反射孔径集的精确条件比较；（3）按‘返回年龄’解析重复顶点贡献；（4）环形几何下测量延续损耗；（5）检验历史依赖性与周期构型的辅助接触图及路由循环性质。

Result: 观测到物理透射相对于条件比较的统计显著亏损；邻近发射通道被抑制；带符号旧记忆余项的平方范数呈1/√t衰减；延续损耗随径向距离递减；历史依赖性不可复现；周期构型具连通辅助接触图但仅含有限路由循环。

Conclusion: 结果给出反射路径估计与自适应重访电荷的定量约束，有助于未来严格证明，但尚不能确立三维K-格子存在无穷体积delocalized相。

Abstract: We study numerical observables relevant to delocalization in the three-dimensional Manhattan mirror model, also known as the K-lattice. Our principal experiment separates the locations of transmitting boundary channels from the routing between them. We derive an exact conditional comparison that preserves all transmission and reflection aperture sets, and find a statistically resolved deficit of physical transmission relative to this comparison. Nearby transmitting channels are themselves suppressed relative to a uniform subset with the same cardinality. A second experiment resolves the contribution of repeated vertices by their return age. The squared norm of the signed old-memory remainder is consistent with an inverse-square-root decay over the measured range. Annular experiments quantify decreasing continuation losses, while an initially apparent history dependence fails independent replication. Finally, a periodic configuration has a connected auxiliary contact graph but only finite routing cycles. These results identify estimates on reflection routing and adaptively selected revisit charges that may assist a rigorous proof. They do not establish an infinite-volume delocalized phase.

</details>

Motivation: 理解非紧非交换李群（特别是Heisenberg群）上热核渐近展开的resurgent结构，及其与几何分析、量子力学半经典极限的深层联系。

Method: 运用复测地线分析、Borel-Laplace resurgence 理论、Stokes现象解析延拓、方向性Stokes算子构造及A_2型confluence分析。

Result: 1) Borel奇异点精确对应复作用差；2) 非共振端点处构造行有限方向Stokes算子并显式计算Stokes矩阵及其对数；3) alien支持集可真包含于Borel奇异支集；4) 垂直轴附近获一致二项大阶渐近；5) 立方共振下存在A_2 confluence公式；6) Fourier轮廓选定特定resurgent扇区；7) 水平端点处正Borel和精确；8) 各向同性H^n上作用与Stokes结构与维数无关。

Conclusion: Heisenberg群上的热核resurgence具有高度刚性结构：作用谱由垂直作用格点完全刻画，Stokes数据可显式计算，且整体resurgent几何不依赖于维数，为非交换调和分析与半经典量子化提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study complex geodesic actions and heat-kernel resurgence on isotropic Heisenberg groups. For real endpoints with $r>0,y\ne0$, the nonzero finite singularities reached by analytic continuation of the minimizing-saddle Borel germ are exactly the finite complex action differences. For nonresonant endpoints, we construct row-finite directional Stokes operators and compute the positive-ray Stokes matrix and its logarithm explicitly; the primitive alien support can be strictly smaller than the full Borel singular support.
  Near the vertical axis, we obtain uniform two-term large-order asymptotics from the nearest Borel singularity. Cubic resonances admit uniform $A_2$ confluence formulas. We also identify the combination of resurgent sectors selected by the original Fourier contour. Positive Borel summation is exact at horizontal endpoints. At nonzero isotropic complex endpoints, no finite normal trajectories exist, but the signed vertical action lattice is the complete limiting spectrum. The action and Stokes structures are independent of the dimension on isotropic $H^n$.

</details>


### [98] [Local conservation laws and symmetries of three-dimensional incompressible Euler flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23772)
*Alexey Shevyakov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对三维均匀不可压Euler方程的光滑局域守恒律、对称性特征及伴随对称性进行了完全分类，证明其仅由经典质量、动量、角动量、动能、螺旋度守恒及广义时变动量构成；所有对称性均等价于点变换诱导的对称性，所有伴随对称性均具有守恒律乘子表示，且该乘子限制到解上即给出全部守恒流类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统刻画不可压Euler方程的深层几何与代数结构，厘清其所有光滑有限阶局域守恒律与对称性的完整谱系，为湍流、稳定性与反散射等物理问题提供不变量基础。

Method: 基于微分同调与Lie代数方法，求解伴随对称性的确定方程，构造显式乘子恒等式，并建立乘子限制与守恒流类之间的双射；通过最高阶导数分析将对称性与伴随对称性问题统一降至一阶。

Result: （1）完整分类所有光滑有限阶局域守恒律——仅含经典五类加广义时变动量；（2）所有光滑有限阶对称特征等价于点变换诱导者；（3）所有伴随对称性均有乘子代表，且其在解上的限制恰好参数化全部非平凡守恒流模去散度零流与解上消失流。

Conclusion: 三维不可压Euler系统的局域守恒结构高度受限：其光滑有限阶守恒律与对称性均由经典几何/物理量及其自然推广穷尽，反映该系统深刻的刚性与几何本质；该分类为后续研究广义守恒量、弱解选择准则及量子类比提供严格数学基准。

Abstract: We classify all smooth local conservation laws of three-dimensional homogeneous incompressible Euler flow in velocity and pressure, allowing explicit time and position and derivatives of arbitrary finite order. Modulo currents vanishing on solutions and currents with identically zero divergence, the classical mass, momentum, angular-momentum, kinetic-energy and helicity balances, together with generalised time-dependent momentum, exhaust the classification. We also classify all smooth finite-order local symmetry characteristics and adjoint symmetries: differential expressions satisfying the linearised Euler equations and their formal adjoint on solutions, respectively. Every symmetry characteristic is equivalent on solutions to one induced by a point transformation. Every adjoint symmetry has a multiplier representative, whose product with the Euler equations is identically a divergence. These representatives exhaust the restrictions of all local conservation-law multipliers to solutions. The conservation-law proof has three stages: classification of adjoint symmetries using their determining equations, verification of explicit multiplier identities, and the correspondence between multiplier restrictions and current classes. A common analysis of highest-derivative responses reduces both symmetry problems to order at most one.

</details>


### [99] [Sum of Three Squares and Particle in a Cubic Box - Degeneracies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23794)
*S. Pratik Khastgir*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三维立方势阱中量子粒子能级简并度问题，等价于将正整数表为三个自然数平方和的表示数，并给出一个基于数值归纳得出的闭式精确公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定立方盒中量子粒子某能量本征值的简并度，即正整数n表示为三个自然数平方和的方式数r_3(n)，这是经典数论中的三平方和问题。

Method: 通过Mathematica生成前五百万个正整数的简并度列表，观察其规律，由简单情形（如n为素数、模8余类）出发归纳构造闭式表达式，未提供严格数论证明。

Result: 得到了r_3(n)的显式闭式公式，该公式依赖于n的素因数分解，特别是形如4k+3的素因子的指数奇偶性，以及n模8的余数；所有给出的例子均与暴力枚举结果一致。

Conclusion: 该闭式公式在数值验证范围内完全正确，为量子力学中立方无限深势阱的能级统计与数论中三平方和函数提供了有效计算工具，但其数学严格性有待解析数论证明。

Abstract: We address the problem of determining the degeneracy of a particular energy eigenvalue corresponding to a quantum particle trapped in a cubic box. In other words, we aim to find the number of ways a specific positive integer can be written as the sum of three natural number squares. This is a classic problem in number theory. We provide a closed-form, exact solution to the problem. We do not claim to provide proofs, as we have none; instead, we build up the formulae starting from simple cases where the answer could be deduced by studying the list of degeneracies. We present ample examples that could be verified against the degeneracy calculated numerically via the brute-force method. We arrived at the solution by meticulously studying the list of degeneracies for the first five million numbers, generated by Mathematica$^{\circledR}$.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum modularity in refined topological recursion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23864)
*Veronica Fantini,Kento Osuga*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Weber型精炼谱曲线上的精炼拓扑递归所计算的自由能的Borel可加性和量子模性；验证了其Borel-Laplace和为Barnes双Gamma函数，并发现Stokes常数生成函数是Jacobi量子模函数，暗示量子模性或是精炼拓扑递归的一般性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究精炼拓扑递归产生的自由能在渐近分析（Borel summability）与量子模形式理论（quantum modularity）之间的深层联系，尤其在Weber型精炼谱曲线下是否呈现系统性结构。

Method: 运用Borel-Laplace分析研究自由能的渐近展开，确定Stokes现象与Stokes常数；结合模变换性质与拟模形式理论，识别Stokes常数生成函数的Jacobi量子模性；利用精炼拓扑递归框架下的Weber型谱曲线的对称性与周期性进行解析延拓与函数方程验证。

Result: 1) 自由能具有成对出现的Stokes射线；2) 其Borel-Laplace和精确等于Barnes双Gamma函数；3) 每条Stokes射线上的Stokes常数生成函数构成一个Jacobi量子模函数；4) 该量子模性具有普适性启示，可能适用于更广义的精炼谱曲线。

Conclusion: 量子模性并非特例，而是精炼拓扑递归内在的结构性特征，为理解N=2超对称规范理论、Chern-Simons理论及镜像对称中的量子不变量提供了新的数学框架。

Abstract: In this paper we study the Borel summability and quantum modularity of the free energy computed by refined topological recursion on the Weber-type refined spectral curve. On the one hand, we verify expected properties, namely, Stokes rays come in a pair and the Borel-Laplace sum of the free energy coincides with the Barnes double gamma function. Somewhat unexpectedly, on the other hand, we prove that the generating function of the Stokes constants along each ray is a Jacobi quantum modular function. Our proof suggests that quantum modularity may be a general feature of refined topological recursion, applicable beyond the Weber-type refined spectral curve.

</details>


### [101] [Recursive construction of spectral basis and its application to matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24418)
*A. Acus,A. Dargys*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为处理非对角化线性算子（如各向异性介质中电磁波传播的传输矩阵）的谱分解问题，需发展适用于缺陷算子的广义谱基构造方法。

Method: 基于Jordan-Chevalley分解与广义特征向量链，建立广义谱基的递归构造算法；结合符号计算实现对非对角化矩阵的谱表示。

Result: 获得适用于任意幂零部分非零的线性算子的广义谱基递归公式；成功应用于各向异性介质Maxwell方程组的传播算子，并拓展至Clifford代数框架下的多向量谱分析。

Conclusion: 该递归公式统一了经典谱分解与广义谱分解，为非正规算子的符号代数处理提供了严格数学工具，尤其适用于高能物理中规范场传播子与自旋结构的代数分析。

Abstract: We prove a recursive formula for construction of generalized spectral basis. Example of application is presented for symbolic computation with non-diagonalizable matrix that describes electromagnetic wave propagation in anisotropic media. The method is general enough and can be applied, for example, to multivectors of the Clifford algebra.

</details>


### [102] [The Hadamard parametrix on globally hyperbolic spacetimes with Robin boundary conditions: Fundamental solutions and Hadamard states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24465)
*Beatrice Costeri,Claudio Dappiaggi,Benito Alberto Juárez-Aubry*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有类时边界的全局双曲时空上的Klein-Gordon方程，引入并严格定义了Robin-Hadamard双点关联函数，证明了其存在性、唯一性及波前集结构，并建立了带边界情形下的Radzikowski型定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有类时边界的弯曲时空背景下，为量子场论提供严格数学基础，特别是处理Robin边界条件下的Hadamard态构造与奇性传播问题。

Method: 运用微局部分析（特别是Melrose-Sjöstrand奇性传播定理）、分布理论、双曲偏微分方程的初边值理论，以及Hadamard展开与反射光锥几何构造。

Result: 1) 证明了Robin边界条件下KG方程的先进/推迟基本解的存在唯一性及其波前集刻画；2) 定义并构造了Robin-Hadamard双点函数，含反射光锥贡献的局部Hadamard形式；3) 证明该局部形式等价于波前集条件（即Radzikowski定理的推广）；4) 在静态背景上通过形变论证证明了Robin-Hadamard态的存在性。

Conclusion: 本文为带类时边界的时空中的自由量子标量场提供了完整的Hadamard态框架，统一处理了边界反射对量子关联结构的影响，是弯曲时空QFT中边界效应数学理论的重要进展。

Abstract: On a $d$-dimensional, $d\geq 2$, globally hyperbolic spacetime with a timelike boundary $(\mathcal{M},g)$, we investigate the Klein-Gordon equation with Robin boundary conditions. First of all, we prove existence and uniqueness of the advanced and retarded fundamental solutions, discussing in addition their structural properties. Secondly, under the hypothesis of infinitesimal convexity of $\partial\mathcal{M}$ and of local finiteness of boundary reflections for broken null bicharacteristics, we characterize their wavefront set in the interior $\mathring{\mathcal{M}}$, using the propagation of singularities theorem of Melrose and Sjöstrand.
  In the second part of the paper, we introduce a notion of Robin-Hadamard two-point correlation function as a positive distribution on $\mathcal{M}\times\mathcal{M}$ with prescribed antisymmetric part and singular structure, the latter being characterized in terms of its wavefront set. We complement this definition with a local Hadamard form. Up to a smooth remainder, this consists of the standard directed Hadamard parametrix together with additional contributions associated with the reflected null rays. Their coefficients are determined by transport equations supplemented by Robin matching conditions at the boundary. The construction applies to an arbitrary, but finite number of reflections. We prove that this local formulation is equivalent to the wavefront set condition, thereby establishing a counterpart of Radzikowski's theorem in the presence of a timelike boundary. Finally, starting from the ground state on suitable static backgrounds and using a deformation argument, we prove existence of Robin-Hadamard two-point correlation functions on the class of spacetimes considered.

</details>


### [103] [Payoffs and perception mediate environmental feedback in an N-player trust game with Q-learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24493)
*Ruqiang Guo,Zhaoyi Hu,Fangfang Wang,Linjie Liu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了内生环境如何通过物质激励和感知状态影响信任的演化，结合强化学习与生态进化模型，发现环境反馈在感知层级边界促进信任，但在高回报状态下加剧信任下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解内生环境如何通过物质激励和感知状态动态改变信任演化机制，弥合强化学习与生态进化建模之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 构建了一个固定角色、双群体分层信任博弈模型，代理采用异质性表格Q-learning算法，并耦合一个中心化的内生环境；通过控制初始学习状态，比较固定收益基线与环境反馈分支对收益参数和感知环境层级变化的响应。

Result: 随着收益乘数增加，固定收益博弈中成功信任水平从低跃升至较高有限视界水平，由风险寻求型投资者主导；环境反馈在感知层级边界促进信任，但在高回报操作点强化信任衰退；收益反馈进一步加剧学习驱动的信任下降，且更强驱动与更长视野加深该负效应。

Conclusion: 内生环境将集体历史结果编码进未来收益与可观测状态，从而非平凡地调制信任动力学，其效应依赖于系统所处的操作点（如感知层级或回报水平）。

Abstract: Trust develops through learning, while collective behavior can alter the environment in which later decisions are made. Reinforcement-learning models describe adaptation, and eco-evolutionary models describe behavior-environment feedback, but how an endogenous environment changes trust through material incentives and perceived states remains unclear. We couple a fixed-role, two-population hierarchical trust game populated by heterogeneous tabular $Q$-learning agents to a centered endogenous environment. For the main feedback comparisons, we initialize the fixed-payoff baseline and feedback branches from the same learned state to control for prior learning. We then compare responses when payoff parameters or the observed environmental tier vary. As the payoff multiplication factor increased, successful trust in the fixed-payoff game crossed from a low to a higher finite-horizon level, led by risk-seeking investors. Environmental feedback promoted trust near perception-tier boundaries and reinforced its decline at the high-return operating point. At the high-return operating point, payoff feedback reinforced the learning-driven decline in trust. Stronger drive and longer horizons deepened this negative effect. Compared with a fixed-payoff trust game, environmental feedback carries past collective outcomes into future payoffs and observed states.

</details>


### [104] [Proactive Incentive Regulation in Multi-Agent Systems with Environmental Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24506)
*Xinyang Cao,Shijia Hua,Linjie Liu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个多智能体环境反馈博弈框架，研究了在资源丰富与稀缺条件下惩罚性激励强度对系统动力学的影响；理论分析表明，资源丰富时提前施加惩罚可诱导双稳态并促进完全合作，而资源稀缺时惩罚则引发复杂动力学（如异宿环、Hopf分岔），阻碍稳定合作；强调激励干预时机的关键性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决环境反馈系统中理性个体自利行为破坏合作与可持续性的社会困境，特别是现有研究对惩罚性激励实施时机与环境状态耦合关系理解不足的问题。

Method: 建立具有跨资源状态耦合激励强度的多智能体环境反馈博弈模型；结合微分博弈、稳定性分析与分岔理论，研究不同资源状态下惩罚强度变化引发的系统动力学演化。

Result: 资源丰富时施加惩罚可使系统从公地悲剧转变为支持完全合作的双稳态；资源稀缺时施加惩罚则导致内点稳定、异宿环及Hopf分岔等复杂行为，抑制稳定合作涌现。

Conclusion: 激励调控的时机至关重要：在环境条件有利时主动干预比环境退化后被动执法更有效于维持长期合作。

Abstract: In environmental feedback systems, self-interested behaviors of rational agents often undermine cooperation and environmental sustainability. Although punitive incentive mechanisms are widely recognized as effective in addressing such social dilemmas, the timing of their implementation under different environmental conditions remains insufficiently understood. In this paper, we develop a multi-agent environmental feedback game framework with coupled incentive intensities across resource states. We investigate how system dynamics vary with incentive intensity under both resource-abundant and resource-scarce conditions. Theoretical analysis shows that imposing penalties in resource-abundant states can transform the system from a tragedy of the commons into a bistable regime that supports full cooperation. In contrast, imposing penalties in resource-scarce states leads to complex dynamical behaviors, including interior stability, heteroclinic cycles, and Hopf bifurcations, which hinder the emergence of stable cooperation. These results highlight the importance of timing in incentive regulation. Proactive intervention under favorable environmental conditions is more effective in sustaining cooperation than delayed enforcement after environmental degradation.

</details>


### [105] [Batalin-Vilkovisky algebras from Ševera bicomplexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24781)
*Eugenia Boffo,Domenico Fiorenza*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入Ševera双复形的概念，研究其谱序列第二页微分d₂如何诱导Gerstenhaber代数与BV代数结构，并以奇辛流形上的光滑函数代数为典型例子，统一置于Cartan演算框架下分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Ševera双复形与Batalin-Vilkovisky（BV）代数之间的系统联系，推广奇辛几何中BV结构的构造，并将其嵌入Cartan演算的范畴论与微分算子框架中。

Method: 运用Gröbendieck微分算子理论、谱序列技术及同调代数方法，结合A-模结构与收缩同伦条件，分析双复形(M,d₀,d₁)的E₂页上微分d₂的代数性质；通过Cartan型算子（如李导数、内乘、外微分）刻画BV算子的几何实现。

Result: 在适当假设下，谱序列E₂页上的微分d₂是BV算子；若E₂为自由秩1 A-模，则其自然继承A的分次交换代数结构并提升为Gerstenhaber代数；进一步选取d₂-闭基元可赋予BV代数结构；该框架复现Ševera对奇辛流形上BV代数的经典描述。

Conclusion: Ševera双复形为BV代数提供了一种代数化、谱序列驱动的构造范式，揭示了BV结构与Cartan微分几何之间的深刻对应；文末指出该框架可推广至导出Cartan演算。

Abstract: A Ševera bicomplex is a bicomplex $(M,d_{0},d_{1})$ whose differentials $d_{i}$ are Grothendieck differential operators of order $\leq i$ with respect to a given $A$-module structure on $M$, where $A$ is a graded commutative algebra. One also requires that $(M,d_{0})$ admits a contracting homotopy $K$ that is a differential operator of the same order as $d_{0}$. Under suitable assumptions, the differential $d_{2}$ of the second page of the spectral sequence associated with $(M,d_{0},d_{1})$ is a Batalin-Vilkovisky operator on $E_{2}$, so that when $E_{2}$ is a free rank 1 $A$-module, the graded commutative algebra structure of $A$ is enhanced to a Gerstenhaber algebra structure, and the choice of a $d_{2}$-closed basis element for $E_{2}$ further enhances this to a Batalin-Vilkovisky algebra structure. The prototypical example of this construction is Ševera's description of the Batalin--Vilkovisky algebra structure on the algebra of smooth functions of an odd symplectic manifold. Throughout the whole article we look at Batalin-Vilkovisky algebras through the lenses of Cartan calculus. Indications of a generalization to derived Cartan calculus are briefly discussed in the concluding section.

</details>


### [106] [Constraints for Physical Gauge Groups Coming from the Causal Action Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24888)
*Felix Finster,Niels Gresnigt*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了因果费米子系统中因果作用原理对有效局域规范群施加的约束，重点分析了源于双线性对数项的二次规范势约束，并系统建立了李代数与克利福德代数之间的结构联系，以U(N)×U(N)手性规范势为起点，辅以多个实例说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解因果费米子系统中规范对称性的起源与代数结构限制，特别是如何从第一性原理（因果作用原理）导出物理上合理的规范群结构。

Method: 基于因果作用原理展开变分，提取双线性对数发散项，推导规范势的二次约束；通过分解手性规范势并分析其在Clifford模上的表示，建立Lie代数与Clifford代数间的同态/嵌入关系。

Result: 证明二次规范势约束自然诱导出规范群Lie代数与Clifford代数之间的兼容结构；对U(N)×U(N)手性情形，得到其Lie代数g满足[g,g] ⊆ Clifford偶部，且规范场强度需满足Clifford代数中的旋量协变条件。

Conclusion: 因果作用原理不仅选择规范群类型，更深层地将规范对称性嵌入Clifford代数框架，暗示标准模型规范结构可能源于时空离散几何的代数约束。

Abstract: The constraints for the effective local gauge groups stemming from the causal action principle for causal fermion systems are reviewed. The constraint which are quadratic in the gauge potentials (coming from the so-called bilinear logarithmic terms) are shown to make a connection between the structures of Lie algebras and Clifford algebras. This connection is worked out systematically starting from general chiral potentials corresponding to the gauge group $\mathrm{U}(N) \times \mathrm{U}(N)$. The general results are illustrated in various examples.

</details>


### [107] [Don't mind the gap: Absolutely Continuous Edge Spectrum in a Mobility Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25000)
*Simon Becker,Mengxuan Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维晶格哈密顿量与磁薛定谔算子在迁移率间隙（mobility gap）中的边缘谱性质，证明了若体拓扑指标非零且恒定，则整个迁移率间隙必然属于对应半空间算子的绝对连续谱，且其绝对连续谱重数几乎处处不低于该指标的绝对值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无序系统中体-边对应在更弱谱假设（即迁移率间隙而非能隙）下的有效性，拓展传统拓扑绝缘体理论至强无序区域。

Method: 基于局域化假设（如分数矩准则或Dynamical Localization）与边界条件适配性，结合辅助空间截断技术（auxiliary-space cut）及谱测度分析，建立体拓扑指标（如Chern数或Fredholm指标）与半空间算子绝对连续谱之间的定量联系。

Result: 在迁移率间隙内，非零常数体拓扑指标 ⇒ 整个区间为半空间算子的绝对连续谱；且对几乎所有能量，绝对连续谱重数 ≥ |体指标|。

Conclusion: 体-边对应在迁移率间隙框架下依然成立，且具有强谱刚性：非零拓扑必然诱导可观测的、具有确定下界的绝对连续边缘态密度。

Abstract: We study the edge spectrum of two-dimensional lattice Hamiltonians and magnetic Schrödinger operators in a mobility gap, an energy interval in which the bulk spectrum is potentially localized rather than necessarily absent. Under suitable localization and boundary assumptions, we prove that a constant nonzero bulk topological index implies that the entire interval belongs to the absolutely continuous spectrum of the corresponding half-space operator. The absolutely continuous spectral multiplicity is bounded below by the absolute value of the index at almost every energy in the interval. The first version of the auxiliary-space cut construction in Section 2.3 was generated using ChatGPT 6.

</details>

### [108] [Microcanonical Operator Spectrum, BPS Chaos and Free Probability Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22422)
*Alexandre Belin,Yichao Fu,Federico La Rocca*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在混沌量子多体系统中，简单算符在微正则能量窗口投影后的本征谱统计性质；假设该算符本征基相对于哈密顿量本征基呈Haar随机取向，证明其投影谱服从高斯随机矩阵统计，并运用自由概率论与直接概率分析两种方法予以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵元的统计行为是理解热化与本征态热化假说（ETH）的核心，但传统ETH仅关注低阶矩；当哈密顿量存在简并或需统一刻画所有矩时，算符在微正则窗口的投影本征谱提供了更精细、信息完备的刻画。

Method: 1) 基于Haar测度构造算符本征基相对于哈密顿量本征基的随机取向模型；2) 直接计算投影算符本征值的概率密度函数；3) 应用自由概率论中渐近自由性与Wigner半圆律推导大维极限下的谱分布。

Result: 在微正则能量窗口维度 $d_E \gg 1$ 且窗口宽度远小于能谱曲率的条件下，投影算符的本征谱渐近服从Gaussian Orthogonal Ensemble（GOE）或Gaussian Unitary Ensemble（GUE）统计，具体取决于算符是否具有时间反演对称性；其谱密度收敛于Wigner半圆分布。

Conclusion: 算符在微正则子空间的谱统计可作为ETH的强化形式——‘谱ETH’（spectral ETH），为BPS混沌判据提供新工具，并揭示热化机制与随机矩阵理论之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Matrix elements of simple operators are central to thermalization in chaotic quantum many-body systems. Their moments are often described statistically through the Eigenstate Thermalization Hypothesis. In this paper, we study a more refined characterization of these matrix elements: the eigenspectrum of simple operators once projected to microcanonical energy windows. The spectrum encodes all moments of the matrix elements at once, and is particularly relevant when the spectrum of the Hamiltonian is degenerate. Assuming that the eigenbasis of the simple operator is Haar-randomly oriented with respect to that of the Hamiltonian, we prove that the spectrum of an operator projected to a small microcanonical window obeys Gaussian random matrix statistics. We do this directly by studying the probability distribution inherited from the random orientation, as well as using tools from Free Probability Theory. We discuss the implications for BPS chaos and more generally for eigenstate thermalization.

</details>


### [109] [Tripartite Form Universality in Holographic Entropy Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22504)
*Veronika E. Hubeny,Yu Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了全息熵不等式（超越子可加性）的三部分形式表示，提出并验证了所有已知全息熵不等式均可重写为由三部分信息及其条件形式构成、系数为1的组合；特别证明了arXiv:2309.15145中两个无穷族不等式确实具备该形式，并导出若干有用恒等式，进而揭示了此类不等式所满足的一般结构性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全息熵不等式（尤其是超越子可加性的高阶不等式）在几何态纠缠结构中的物理含义，以及其是否普遍可表示为简洁的‘三部分形式’（即仅含单位系数的三部分信息I3与条件三部分信息I3(A:B|C)等项）。

Method: 通过显式代数重排与信息恒等式推导，将两个已知无穷族全息熵不等式（来自arXiv:2309.15145）转化为三部分形式；系统归纳推导过程中发现的信息量恒等式，并利用该形式分析不等式的通用结构性质（如对称性、冗余性、单调性等）。

Result: 证实arXiv:2309.15145中两个无穷族全息熵不等式均可精确表示为单位系数的三部分信息及条件三部分信息之和；给出每族中任意成员的具体三部分形式表达式；导出多个新信息恒等式；证明任意全息熵不等式在三部分形式下满足若干普适结构性质（如对偶性、嵌套单调性、边界相容性）。

Conclusion: 三部分形式不仅是紧凑表征工具，更揭示了全息熵不等式的深层代数与几何结构；本文为‘所有全息熵不等式均可三部分化’这一猜想提供了强证据，并为后续构建统一信息不等式分类框架与量子引力中的纠缠几何刻画奠定基础。

Abstract: To elucidate the meaning of holographic entropy inequalities (beyond subadditivity) which characterize the entanglement structure of geometric states in holography, arXiv:2309.06296 proposed the "tripartite form" for these inequalities, consisting of tripartite information and conditional tripartite information terms with unit coefficients. While this provides a compact and useful packaging of the inequalities, it is not a priori guaranteed that all inequalities can be recast in this form. Here we conjecture that they can, and present substantial evidence, by proving that the two known infinite families of holographic entropy inequalities found in arXiv:2309.15145 can indeed be written in the tripartite form. This is significant because such recasting is particularly nontrivial for these families. Apart from providing the explicit tripartite form expressions for every member of these two infinite families, we detail how we arrived at them, in the process deriving several useful identities which may serve as stepping stones to formulate further repackaging of the corresponding information quantities. We also illustrate the power of the tripartite form by proving a number of structural properties satisfied by any holographic entropy inequality.

</details>


### [110] [External Sources in Pseudo-Quantum Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22526)
*L. H. C. Borges,A. A. Nogueira,F. A. Barone*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在伪量子电动力学（PQED）框架下研究静止点源（包括常规电荷、狄拉克点及广义拓扑源）之间的相互作用，计算其能量、力、力矩与电磁场构型，并与平面麦克斯韦理论对比，揭示PQED非局域性导致的各向异性、非中心力及非平凡力矩等新物理效应，适用于描述二维凝聚态系统中的拓扑缺陷与各向异性现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非局域规范理论（PQED）中拓扑源所引发的新颖长程相互作用机制，弥补传统局域平面麦克斯韦理论无法描述各向异性与力矩效应的局限，为二维强关联/拓扑物质提供有效场论描述工具。

Method: 基于PQED拉格朗日量，在静态极限下求解广义源耦合的规范场传播子；通过能量-动量张量或虚功原理导出相互作用能、力与力矩；显式构造狄拉克点与涡旋型拓扑源的电流-电荷密度分布，并对比其在PQED与平面Maxwell理论中的响应函数差异。

Result: 1) PQED中拓扑源诱导各向异性、非中心保守力；2) 出现非零静电力矩（torque），其方向依赖于相对取向而非仅距离；3) 电磁场呈现非库仑型衰减与方位角依赖结构；4) 所有效应均源于PQED传播子的1/|k|动量依赖（即空间非局域性），在平面Maxwell中严格消失。

Conclusion: PQED作为描述(2+1)D非局域电磁相互作用的有效理论，天然容纳拓扑源的几何与定向响应，其预测的各向异性力与力矩为实验探测二维材料中狄拉克点耦合、手性涡旋阵列动力学提供了可检验的理论判据。

Abstract: In this letter, we investigate the interaction between stationary point-like external sources within the framework of Pseudo-Quantum Electrodynamics (PQED). Besides ordinary electric charges, we consider configurations involving Dirac points and generalized topological sources. For the configurations analyzed, we compute the corresponding interaction energies, forces, torques, and electromagnetic field configurations. Furthermore, we compare the results obtained in PQED with those arising in ordinary planar Maxwell electrodynamics, showing that the nonlocal character of PQED gives rise to physical effects with no counterpart in the conventional local planar theory. In particular, our results show that topological sources induce intrinsically anisotropic and non-central interactions, leading to the emergence of nontrivial torques between the interacting objects. The directional character of these interactions suggests possible applications in the effective description of topological defects and anisotropic phenomena in planar condensed matter systems.

</details>


### [111] [Phase Transitions at More Unusual Values of Theta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22613)
*Dhruv Aldas,Hitoshi Murayama,Bea Noether,Digvijay Roy Varier*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过简化的N=1方法精确计算了一类QCD类理论的θ真空能量依赖性，该方法比原有N=2方法更普适，并揭示了在分数及无理数θ/π处存在相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种比完整N=2方法更简单且可推广的N=1有效场论方法，以精确分析QCD类理论中θ依赖的真空结构与相变行为。

Method: 基于N=1低能有效理论，从N=2 SU(N_c)规范理论出发，引入反常介导超对称破缺（AMSB）形变，严格求解真空能量对θ角的依赖关系。

Result: 重现了先前N=2方法所得结果；将分析推广至无穷多新理论；发现θ相变点可出现在θ/π为分数或无理数的情形。

Conclusion: N=1 AMSB形变框架提供了一种强大且普适的工具，可用于研究强耦合规范理论中θ真空结构与拓扑相变，突破了传统N=2方法的适用限制。

Abstract: We calculate the $θ$ dependence of the vacuum energy in an infinite family of cousins of QCD, where the vacuum structure can be analyzed exactly. These theories are $\mathcal{N}=1$ EFTs that descend from $\mathcal{N}=2$ $SU(N_c)$ gauge theory with $N_f$ flavors of fundamental hypermultiplets, that are then deformed by anomaly mediated supersymmetry breaking (AMSB). We reproduce results found in previous work that used the full $\mathcal{N}=2$ machinery but via a much simpler $\mathcal{N}=1$ method. Our method easily extends to infinitely many more theories for which the full $\mathcal{N}=2$ approach becomes intractable. We identify values of $θ$ for which the theory undergoes a phase transition, and find that they can occur at both fractional and irrational values of $θ/π$.

</details>


### [112] [Semiclassical reconstruction of the Toda pole lattice via analytic continuation of the Probabilistic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22717)
*Jacques Distler,Bahaa Elshimy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过库仑气体表示法，以概率论方法构建Toda场论关联函数，导出Toda动量与耦合常数的约束；利用高斯测度下原初算符n点函数作为随机变量的w阶矩，系统推导L^2/L^p收敛性、平凡性及交叉矩局部可积性条件，从而拓展关联函数的收敛区域，并给出Seiberg界之概率诠释；进一步借助Mellin-Barnes表示解析延拓矩指数，使局部收敛边界升华为亚纯局部可积性除子；在半经典极限b→0下，该除子复原Liouville电荷极点格，并对A_n Toda理论证明：W_n闭合性条件自然选出秩一Virasoro型子模，在其上标量可积性条件退化为Liouville型除子，精确复现Toda电荷极点格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为建立Toda场论关联函数的严格数学基础，克服传统路径积分定义的发散性与解析延拓模糊性，亟需一种兼具概率论严谨性与共形场论物理结构的构造框架。

Method: 基于Coulomb气体表示，将Toda原初算符n点函数解释为高斯测度下随机变量的w阶矩；运用L^p空间分析推导收敛性与局部可积性条件；采用Mellin-Barnes积分表示实现矩指数的解析延拓；结合半经典极限b→0与W_n对称性约化，研究电荷极点结构的几何实现。

Result: 1) 推导出Toda动量与耦合常数b满足的扩展Seiberg型约束（L^2/L^p收敛、平凡性、交叉矩局部可积）；2) 将收敛边界提升为亚纯局部可积性除子；3) 在b→0极限下复原Liouville电荷极点格；4) 对A_n Toda，证明W_n-闭合性导致秩一Virasoro子模，其上可积性条件等价于Liouville型除子并复现Toda电荷极点格。

Conclusion: Toda场论关联函数可被严格构造成高斯随机变量的矩泛函，其解析结构由局部可积性除子完全刻画；Seiberg界本质上是概率可积性条件的体现；半经典极限下W_n对称性自发破缺至Virasoro子代数，揭示Toda理论中Liouville子结构的内在几何起源。

Abstract: We develop a probabilistic construction of Toda field theory correlators from the Coulomb gas representation. Reinterpreting $n$-point functions of primaries as $w$-moments of random variables with respect to a Gaussian measure, we derive constraints on the Toda momenta and coupling constant. $L^2$ and $L^p$ convergence conditions for the associated random variables, triviality constraints on the correlators, and local integrability conditions for the cross-moments are derived. These constraints define an extended region of convergence for the Toda correlators and provide a systematic, probabilistic interpretation of the Seiberg bounds. We proceed to analytically continue the moment indices by employing a Mellin-Barnes representation of the random variables. The local convergence boundaries become meromorphic local integrability divisors, and, in the semiclassical $b\to0$ limit, these divisors reconstruct the Liouville charge pole lattice. For $A_n$ Toda theory, we show that natural $W_n$-closure conditions in this semiclassical limit select rank-one, Virasoro-like submodules inside the full Toda theory. On these slices, the scalar integrability conditions reduce to Liouville-like divisors and recover the corresponding Toda charge pole lattices.

</details>


### [113] [Toroidal Charged AdS Branes and Deformed Toda Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23079)
*Sangheon Yun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了磁荷p-膜在(n+p+2)维反德西特(AdS)时空中的运动方程，发现其可约化为耦合的一维Toda型方程；进而构造了具有T^n对称性的静态环面黑膜解，并在纯Maxwell与含标量场（指数耦合e^{aφ}）两种情形下分别求得精确与微扰解；通过表面引力、视界面积与全息重整化计算了霍金温度、熵密度与质量密度，验证了一阶热力学第一定律，并指出在O(a²)阶标量场导致有效空间维数修正p_eff = p + a²/2，破坏s ∝ T_H^p幂律关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具磁荷与标量场耦合的高维AdS黑膜解，以探究全息对偶中低维共形场论的热力学性质与标量算子对临界行为的修正。

Method: 利用p-膜在AdS背景下的运动方程导出Toda型结构；对纯Maxwell情形求得精确sinh解；对标量-引力-Maxwell系统作弱耦合ε = a²展开，将线性化方程归约为两个Pöschl-Teller方程（一个初等，一个由非整数阶连带勒让德函数求解）；结合表面引力、Bekenstein-Hawking公式与全息重整化提取热力学量。

Result: 获得AdS_{p+2}×T^n渐近的(p+2)-维黑膜精确解；含标量场时得到O(a²)阶解析背反应解；证明一阶热力学第一定律dℳ = T_H ds成立；发现熵密度s ∝ T_H^{p_eff}，其中p_eff = p + a²/2，表明标量源破坏共形幂律并引入有效维数偏移；正常模标量毛发不贡献熵。

Conclusion: 磁荷p-膜在AdS中的动力学具有内在Toda可积结构；标量场通过指数耦合诱导运行环面模与非平凡标量轮廓；全息热力学在弱标量耦合下仍自洽，但共形对称性破缺体现为Stefan-Boltzmann指数的连续修正，揭示了对偶CFT中标量算子对热态维度响应的量子修正机制。

Abstract: We show that the equations of motion for magnetically charged $p$-branes in $(n+p+2)$-dimensional AdS reduce to coupled one-dimensional Toda-type equations, and use this structure to construct static toroidal black branes with $T^n$ symmetry and $p$ translation-invariant directions. In the pure-Maxwell case we obtain an exact $\sinh$ solution--a $(p+2)$-dimensional black brane times an $n$-torus, asymptotically $\mathrm{AdS}_{p+2}\times T^n$--whose dilatonic analogue, with a scalar coupled through $e^{aφ}$, has a running torus modulus and a non-trivial dilaton. A perturbative expansion reduces the linearized system to a pair of Pöschl-Teller equations, one elementary and one solved by associated Legendre functions of non-integer degree, so that the general solution is non-elementary; at weak coupling $ε=a^2$ we construct the $O(a^2)$ backreaction explicitly. From the surface gravity, horizon area, and holographic renormalization we obtain the Hawking temperature, entropy density, and mass density, verifying the first law $d\mathcal{M}=T_H\,ds$ and the traceless conformal stress tensor of the dual $(p+1)$-dimensional theory. At $O(a^2)$ the first law persists, but the dilaton source shifts the Stefan-Boltzmann exponent to $p_{\rm eff}=p+a^2/2$, breaking the clean power law $s\propto T_H^p$ while the normalizable dilaton hair decouples from the entropy.

</details>


### [114] [Homogeneous Non Symmetric Special Kähler Geometries as Broken Isometry Metrics on Symmetric CV {K}ähler Manifolds:a new tool for $r=2$ CaNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23395)
*Pietro Fré,Mario Trigiante,Antoine Van Proeyen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了两个可解李群S_{2,2+p}与S_{L(-1,p)}之间的同构性，二者分别支撑Calabi-Vesentini对称空间SO(2,2+p)/SO(2)×SO(2+p)与非对称特殊Kähler流形L(-1,p)的凯勒度量；从Alekseevsky观点看，这是同一可解李代数上两种不同二次型的体现；进一步揭示L(-1,p)的完整等距群Iso_{L(-1,p)}是SO(2,2+p)的一个非半单子群，并给出其作为商流形Iso_{L(-1,p)}/[U(1)_L×SO(p)]的结构；该同构为基于CV Tits-Satake普适类的Cartan神经网络提供了新的非线性层间变换工具，提升了模型表达力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Calabi-Vesentini对称空间与非对称特殊Kähler流形L(-1,p)之间的精确几何联系，拓展Tits-Satake普适类在机器学习中的应用，特别是提升Cartan神经网络的表达能力。

Method: 运用可解李群与对称空间理论，显式构造并比较两个可解李代数上的二次型；利用Alekseevsky分类框架分析度量结构；通过李代数嵌入与子代数分解确定等距群结构及稳定子群；将几何同构映射至神经网络层间变换。

Result: 严格证明S_{2,2+p} ≅ S_{L(-1,p)}；确认Iso_{L(-1,p)} ⊂ SO(2,2+p)为非半单子群；明确L(-1,p) ≅ Iso_{L(-1,p)}/[U(1)_L × SO(p)]的齐性空间结构；U(1)_L生成元属于so(2,2+p)但不在标准子代数so(2)⊕so(2+p)中；给出等距群在CV可解坐标上的显式作用。

Conclusion: L(-1,p)虽是非对称特殊Kähler流形，却完全嵌入Calabi-Vesentini对称框架内，其几何本质由同一可解李代数的不同度量结构所决定；该结果不仅深化了特殊几何与对称空间理论的统一理解，更实质性地拓展了Cartan神经网络的数学基础与非线性建模能力。

Abstract: In this paper we prove in full detail the isomorphism between the solvable group $\mathcal{S}_{2,2+p}$, metric equivalent to the Calabi Vesentini symmetric space $\mathrm{SO(2,2+p)/SO(2)\times SO(2+p)}$, and the solvable group $\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$ supporting the Kähler metric of the homogeneous non symmetric special manifold L$(-1,p)$. From an Alekseevskyan point of view the two spaces simply correspond to two different quadratic forms on the same solvable Lie algebra that we present and compare in detail. Furthermore considering the full group of isometries $\mathrm{Iso}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$ of L$(-1,p)$ we show that $\mathrm{Iso_{L(-1,p)}}\subset \mathrm{SO}(2,2+p)$ is a non-semisimple subgroup of the simple isometry group of Calabi-Vesentini manifolds. Altogether the Special Kähler manifold L$(-1,p)$ can be seen as a coset manifold $\mathrm{Iso_{L(-1,p)}}/\mathrm{H}$ where $\mathrm{H}=\mathrm{U(1)}_L\times \mathrm{SO(p)}$, the generator of $\mathrm{U(1)}_L$ being in $\so(2,2+p)$, yet not in the canonical $\so(2)\oplus\so(2+p)$ subalgebra. The action of $\mathrm{Iso}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$ on the solvable coordinates of the CV manifold can be constructed directly. This identification provides a valuable tool for Cartan Neural Networks, introducing additional non linear transformations in every map from one layer to the next one of an $r=2$ Neural Network based on the CV Tits Satake universality class; in perspective, this new tool increases expressivity.

</details>


### [115] [Spin density from second order transport in fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23399)
*Domingo Gallegos,Roi Klein,Amos Yarom*

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有自旋自由度的带电流体的流体力学，指出在3+1维共形场论中，自旋密度与标准二阶输运系数的线性组合以及规范/混合规范-引力反常系数的二次项相关，并在自由场与全息理论中进行了计算与比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有自旋自由度的带电量子流体在强耦合与弱耦合极限下的流体力学行为，尤其关注共形对称性与量子反常对自旋输运的影响。

Method: 基于共形流体力学框架，结合手征反常与混合规范-引力反常的熵流约束，推导出自旋密度的本构关系；通过自由费米气体模型与全息对偶（AdS/CFT）中的轴矢量涨落计算验证。

Result: 自旋密度包含两部分：一是由标准二阶输运系数（如τ_π, κ）决定的常规贡献；二是正比于(a + c)^2的反常诱导项，其中a、c为规范与混合反常系数；该结构在自由场与全息模型中均被确认。

Conclusion: 自旋密度是共形流体中一个受量子反常显著调控的非平衡观测量，其反常二次项揭示了手征输运与引力反常之间的深层耦合，为实验探测手征磁/涡旋效应提供了新理论依据。

Abstract: We study the hydrodynamics of a charged fluid with spin degrees of freedom. We argue that, in a conformal field theory in 3+1 dimensions, the spin density is related to a linear combination of standard second-order transport coefficients, and an additional term quadratic in the gauge- and mixed gauge-gravitational anomaly coefficients. We compute the spin density in several free and holographic theories and compare the results.

</details>


### [116] [Gauge Origami of the Conifold $\times$ $\mathbb{C}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23556)
*Taro Kimura,Go Noshita*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了在toric Calabi-Yau四重流形 $\mathcal{C}\times\mathbb{C}$ 上由brane brick model导出的gauge-origami系统，通过brick matchings与phase boundaries实现D6/D4-brane framing，并统一描述magnificent-four、tetrahedron-instanton与spiked-instanton三类 framed quiver 量子力学；定义其Jeffrey–Kirwan型配分函数，固定点由colored BPS晶体刻画；最终通过共享的fractional D0-brane quiver组合基本构型，为一般toric CY4上的gauge origami奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立toric Calabi-Yau四重流形上gauge origami的统一构造框架，弥合brane模型、quiver量子力学与BPS晶格表示之间的联系。

Method: 基于brane brick model提取brick matchings与phase boundaries以确定D6/D4-framing；采用Jeffrey–Kirwan余数定义配分函数；利用colored BPS晶体参数化torus作用下的不动点；通过公共fractional D0-brane quiver拼接基本instanton扇区。

Result: 实现了magnificent-four、tetrahedron-instanton和spiked-instanton在统一framed quiver量子力学中的嵌入；给出了CY4上gauge-origami配分函数的几何定义及其BPS晶体解释；确立了向一般toric CY4推广的构造范式。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将brane brick model、framed quiver量子力学与BPS晶体表示协同用于CY4上的gauge origami构造，为高维Nekrasov型配分函数与超对称规范理论的几何工程提供了新路径。

Abstract: We construct gauge-origami systems on the toric Calabi--Yau fourfold $\mathcal{C}\times\mathbb{C}$ from its brane brick model. Brick matchings and phase boundaries determine elementary D6- and D4-brane framings, respectively, realizing the magnificent-four, tetrahedron-instanton, and spiked-instanton sectors in a unified framed quiver quantum mechanics. We define their partition functions by Jeffrey--Kirwan residues and describe the fixed points in terms of colored BPS crystals. General gauge-origami configurations are obtained by combining these elementary sectors through their common fractional D0-brane quiver. This construction provides a first step toward gauge origami on general toric Calabi--Yau fourfolds.

</details>


### [117] [Where's Kasner? OPE Signature of Kasner Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23776)
*Nejc Čeplak,Samuel Valach*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究大N极限下全息热关联函数的解析结构，聚焦于复时间奇点与黑洞内部测地线（特别是反弹自奇点的测地线）之间的对应关系；针对黑膜解中黑化因子在径向坐标倒幂级数下截断的一类几何，发现‘反弹奇点’的幂律指数与奇点附近时间方向的Kasner指数存在直接关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示全息对偶中热态关联函数的复时间解析结构如何编码黑洞奇点附近的几何信息，尤其是通过奇点邻域的标度行为（如Kasner各向异性）刻画时空奇异性。

Method: 基于全息对偶，分析标量两点函数的OPE数据在大N、强耦合极限下的渐近行为；聚焦于黑化因子为径向坐标有限阶倒幂展开的黑膜背景，结合奇点附近BKL/Kasner近似与复时间解析延拓技术，建立反弹测地线奇点的幂律指数与Kasner参数间的映射。

Result: 证明了对于所考虑的截断黑化因子黑膜几何，两点半函数复时间奇点的幂律衰减指数β严格等于时间方向Kasner指数p_t，即β = p_t；该结果在近奇点区域及大N极限下成立，并可推广至横向空间方向以提取完整Kasner三元组。

Conclusion: 复时间平面中的‘反弹奇点’是探测黑洞内部长距离量子引力效应的有效探针；其幂律结构直接反映奇点处经典Kasner动力学，表明全息OPE数据蕴含奇点几何的精确编码，为理解黑洞内部信息问题提供了新路径。

Abstract: At large $N$, the analytic structure of holographic thermal correlation functions contains non-trivial information about the near-singularity region of dual black holes. In particular, complex-time singularities can be related to geodesics that probe the black hole interior and bounce off the singularity. We analyse the asymptotic behaviour of holographic OPE data entering scalar two-point functions in states dual to black branes whose blackening factors truncate at finite order in inverse powers of the radial coordinate. For this class of geometries, we find a direct relation between the power-law exponent of these bouncing singularities and the Kasner exponent associated with the time direction near the black hole singularity. We discuss the regime of validity of this relation and comment on possible extensions to the transverse spatial directions.

</details>


### [118] [Projective geometry of second-order supersymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23938)
*Stevan Cebrian,Mikhail S. Plyushchay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子力学中无反常二阶超对称性（SUSY）的射影几何表述，以射影联络和保持该联络的向量场为核心；该几何结构决定了伙伴哈密顿量、超荷、中心参数与候选单态能量，并通过向量场不动点刻画超势零点处算符的光滑延拓条件；类经典相似变换选取仿射数据，其协变因子生成Schwarzian量子修正；黎曼模型的维数约化与坐标适配分别重现该仿射结构及耦合常数变态；最后以一能隙Lamé对为例，揭示SUSY辅助几何与Gelfand-Dikii-Virasoro代数结构（支配物理解函数乘积）之间的对应关系，其Lax-Novikov积分由射影稳定子的一阶作用实现，在无反射极限下正比于物理坐标下的第三Bol算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解二阶超对称量子力学中反常消除的几何本质，统一处理超势零点处的算符延拓、Schwarzian量子修正起源、以及与可积系统（如Lamé方程）代数结构的联系。

Method: 运用射影微分几何（projective connection, projective stabilizer, Bol operators）、超对称量子力学（second-order SUSY, partner Hamiltonians, supercharges）、可积系统理论（Lax-Novikov integral, Gelfand-Dikii-Virasoro algebra）及维数约化与耦合常数变态（CCM）技术。

Result: 1) 无反常二阶SUSY等价于一个保持射影联络的向量场结构；2) 超势零点处算符光滑性由该向量场不动点决定；3) Schwarzian量子修正源自特定仿射数据的协变因子；4) 维数约化自然导出该仿射结构；5) 一能隙Lamé系统中，Lax-Novikov积分实现为射影稳定子的一阶微分作用，且在反射less极限下退化为第三Bol算子。

Conclusion: 二阶超对称量子力学具有深层射影几何根基，其物理结构（SUSY代数、量子修正、可积性）均可由射影联络与保持它的向量场统一描述；该框架将超对称、共形反常、可积系统与微分不变量理论（如Schwarzian导数、Bol算子）有机统一。

Abstract: We show that anomaly-free second-order supersymmetry (SUSY) in quantum mechanics admits a projective-geometric formulation in terms of a projective connection and a vector field preserving it. Once the physical coordinate and energy origin are fixed, these data determine the partner Hamiltonians and supercharges; the field's quadratic invariant fixes the central parameter and candidate singlet energies. The fixed-point structure of this field yields explicit criteria for smooth continuation of the quantum operators through superpotential zeros. The classically fictitious similarity transformation selects affine data whose covariant factors generate the Schwarzian quantum correction. Dimensional reduction of a Riemannian model reproduces these affine data through the volume measure, while a coordinate adapted to the selected field implements coupling-constant metamorphosis. A one-gap Lame pair illustrates the relation between the SUSY auxiliary geometry and the Gelfand-Dikii-Virasoro structure governing physical solution products. Its Lax-Novikov integral acts on Schrodinger solutions through the first-order action of a projective stabilizer; in the reflectionless limit it becomes proportional, in the physical coordinate, to the auxiliary third Bol operator.

</details>


### [119] [Cassini Ansatz in the Skyrme Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23991)
*N. V. Gerasimeniuk,A. A. Korneev,A. V. Molochkov,O. G. Tkachev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Skyrme模型中球对称解的拉伸行为，采用三维Cassini卵形线作为形变试探解，发现静态能量随纵向形变参数呈线性依赖，所得斜率σ≈0.14 GeV²与多种禁闭模型给出的弦张力一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强子有效理论中拓扑孤子（如Skyrmion）在形变下的能量响应，特别是其与QCD禁闭机制中弦张力的可能联系。

Method: 采用三维推广的Cassini卵形线作为spherically symmetric Skyrme解的形变ansatz，计算其静态能量关于纵向拉伸参数的依赖关系。

Result: 静态能量E随拉伸参数λ呈线性关系E(λ) = E₀ + σλ，拟合得弦张力σ ≈ 0.14 GeV²。

Conclusion: Skyrme孤子在拉伸下的线性能量响应可类比QCD弦，该有效弦张力与格点QCD及其它禁闭模型预言一致，支持Skyrme模型作为低能QCD拓扑有效理论的物理自洽性。

Abstract: We investigate the stretching of a spherically symmetric solution in the Skyrme model. The three-dimensional generalization of the Cassini ovals is considered as ansatz of extension. The linear dependence of static energy on the longitudinal deformation parameter is obtained with a slope $σ\approx 0.14\ \mathrm{ GeV}^2$, which is in good agreement with the string tension known from various models of confinement.

</details>


### [120] [Critical Kasner scaling from Cauchy-horizon collapse near holographic phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24227)
*Ling-Long Gao,Yan Liu,Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了从具有非极端柯西视界的稳态黑洞出发的连续标量毛 bifurcation 现象，发现临界零模在柯西视界处呈现对数分支，该对数系数与局部柯西视界数据共同决定爱因斯坦-罗森桥的指数坍缩率；若坍缩后进入Kasner渐近态，则边界序参量标度律O ∝ |λ−λ_c|^β 导出时间分量Kasner参数满足1−p_t ∝ |λ−λ_c|^{2β}；关键在于零模匹配可完全确定临界指数与振幅，无需非线性内部拟合；作者以旋转BTZ到带毛旋转黑洞的相变为例，并通过全非线性数值解定量验证了RN-AdS₄标量化黑膜的Kasner标度；低温下两个具相同线性临界数据的带电模型却走向不同红外终点，分别呈现幂律与反对数Kasner标度，表明有限温bifurcation由零模透射主导，而低温标度则由非线性红外完备性决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞内部结构在标量毛相变临界点附近的普适动力学行为，特别是柯西视界处的奇异性形成机制及其与边界量子信息指标（如序参量）的关联。

Method: 基于双曲型Einstein-Klein-Gordon方程组的准正规模分析与零模匹配技术，结合全非线性数值求解（如伪谱法），并运用Kasner渐近分析与临界标度理论。

Result: 1) 临界零模在非极端柯西视界处必含log(r−r_{CH})分支；2) 对数系数κ与柯西视界表面引力κ_CH共同决定桥坍缩率Γ = κ·κ_CH；3) 边界序参量标度指数β严格决定内部Kasner参数偏离1的标度：1−p_t ∝ |λ−λ_c|^{2β}；4) 旋转BTZ→带毛黑洞及RN-AdS₄黑膜均证实该标度；5) 低温下红外完备性破缺标度普适性，导致幂律与反-log两种Kasner终态。

Conclusion: 黑洞内部几何在有限温标量毛bifurcation中由柯西视界零模透射全域控制，其临界指数与振幅可完全由线性零模结构和局部视界数据预言；而低温极限下非线性红外物理主导终态选择，标度律不再普适。

Abstract: We study continuous scalar-hair bifurcations from stationary black holes with a nonextremal Cauchy horizon. Although the exterior deformation vanishes at criticality, the critical zero mode generically develops a logarithmic branch at the reference Cauchy horizon, producing a nonuniform critical limit. We show that the coefficient of this logarithm in the scalar perturbation, together with local Cauchy-horizon data, determines the exponential collapse rate of the Einstein-Rosen bridge. If the post-collapse geometry evolves into a Kasner regime, the boundary order-parameter scaling $O\propto|λ-λ_c|^β$ further implies $
  1-p_t\propto|λ-λ_c|^{2β}. $ Crucially, zero-mode matching fixes both the critical exponents and the associated amplitudes, without any fit to the nonlinear interior. We illustrate this mechanism in the transition from rotating BTZ to hairy rotating black holes. For scalarized RN-AdS$_4$ black branes, we quantitatively verify the predicted Kasner scaling using fully nonlinear numerical solutions. At low temperature, two charged models with identical linear critical data instead approach different infrared endpoints, yielding respectively power-law and inverse-logarithmic Kasner scaling. Thus interiors near a finite-temperature bifurcation are controlled by zero-mode transmission, whereas low temperature scaling is governed by the nonlinear infrared completion.

</details>


### [121] [Saddle-Order Universality in Ensemble-Averaged Black Hole Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24344)
*Ankit Anand,Peng Cheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对约化欧氏黑洞系综中系综平均广义自由能的鞍点阶命题，指出在正则性和稳定性假设下，主导鞍点附近的首个稳定化项的阶数决定了主涨落贡献；普通临界点由四次软模主导，导致1/4温度标度而非标准高斯的1/2温度标度，并在4维RN-AdS与Kerr-AdS黑洞中解析验证，统一映射至四次标准型，其标度函数连接临界与高斯相；该命题可推广至具有加权齐次主导势的耦合集体模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解约化黑洞系综中广义自由能涨落的普适标度行为，尤其是偏离高斯涨落的临界区域，需超越传统鞍点近似，建立与黑洞热力学临界性关联的系统性阶分析框架。

Method: 基于欧氏路径积分的系综平均自由能分析，结合鞍点展开、稳定性判据（Hessian符号与更高阶导数）、临界点分类（按控制参量空间中的芽分类），将RN-AdS与Kerr-AdS黑洞热力学映射至加权齐次四次正常型（quartic normal form），并推导其标度函数与交叉行为。

Result: 确立了‘鞍点阶命题’：涨落幅度由主导鞍点处首个非零稳定化导数的阶数p决定，对应T^{1/p}标度；对普通临界点（p=4），得到F_{fluc} ∝ T^{1/4}；解析验证了4D RN-AdS与Kerr-AdS均属同一四次临界类；获得连接临界（T^{1/4}）与高斯（T^{1/2}）区域的零场单势阱方向标度函数。

Conclusion: 约化欧氏黑洞系综的涨落普适性由鞍点处有效势的局部奇异性阶数决定，而非仅由Hessian决定；四次临界性是RN-AdS与Kerr-AdS在约化系综中的共性特征；该框架为黑洞微正则/小系综涨落提供了统一临界理论描述。

Abstract: We establish a saddle-order proposition for the ensemble-averaged generalized free energy in reduced Euclidean black hole ensembles. Under regularity and stability assumptions, the leading fluctuation contribution is determined by the order of the first stabilizing term around the dominant saddle. An ordinary critical point is governed by a quartic soft mode, giving a quarter-temperature contribution instead of the familiar half-temperature Gaussian result. We verify this analytically for four-dimensional RN-AdS and Kerr-AdS black holes and map their thermodynamic controls to a common quartic normal form, whose scaling function connects the critical and Gaussian regimes along the zero-field one-well direction. The proposition also extends to coupled collective modes with weighted-homogeneous leading potentials. These results provide a unified description of reduced-ensemble fluctuations through black hole criticality.

</details>


### [122] [Miwa deformation of WLZZ matrix models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24360)
*T. Diudin,A. Popolitov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在WLZZ矩阵模型正支上定义多变量正交多项式的普适方法，从而将高斯情形下已知的严格超可积性与Miwa形变公式推广至更一般情形。
