  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展WLZZ矩阵模型中正交多项式的构造方法，以超越已有高斯情形的限制，并推广严格超可积性与Miwa形变结构。

Method: 基于WLZZ矩阵模型正支的测度构造，建立多变量正交多项式的通用Gram-Schmidt正交化框架，并结合特征值积分表示与对称函数理论实现Miwa变量引入。

Result: 确立了WLZZ正支上任意权函数下的多变量正交多项式系存在性与唯一性；证明其Schur展开系数满足严格超可积性（即平均值等于对应Schur函数）；导出适用于一般权函数的Miwa形变微分方程族。

Conclusion: WLZZ矩阵模型正支上的严格超可积性并非高斯特例，而是由正交多项式系的代数结构与Miwa参数化所决定的普适性质；该框架为研究非高斯矩阵模型的可积结构与规范/引力对偶提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this paper we propose a universal way to define multivariate orthogonal polynomials for the positive branch of WLZZ matrix models. This allows us to generalize from the Gaussian case, previously considered in the literature, the strict superintegrability property and the Miwa deformation formulas.

</details>


### [123] [Spin-curvature effects in slowly rotating black hole spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24364)
*Arpita Jana,Subhajit Mazumdar,Sunandan Gangopadhyay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了慢旋转黑洞时空中自旋-曲率相互作用对质量性自旋1/2量子粒子的影响，通过共动标架与WKB近似结合Gordon分解，计算出至线性阶的自旋-曲率量子力，并发现黑洞旋转引入一个比Schwarzschild情形衰减更快的径向力修正项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空（特别是黑洞背景）中，自旋-曲率耦合会显著影响自旋1/2粒子的动力学，而平直时空下该效应消失；需定量刻画该非平庸量子引力效应。

Method: 在Kerr度规弱旋转极限（a ≪ M）下构造粒子共动正交标架（tetrad），代入Dirac方程并利用WKB近似分离快变相位，再通过Gordon分解提取概率流的力学部分（即自旋-曲率量子力），保留至O(a)线性项。

Result: 得到自旋-曲率量子力的显式表达式，其径向分量包含一个由角动量参数a诱导的新项，形式为∝ a/(r^4)（或更陡峭），相较Schwarzschild情形的∝ 1/r^3衰减更快。

Conclusion: 黑洞旋转通过自旋-曲率耦合引入新的、短程主导的量子力学偏转力，表明旋转几何对自旋量子粒子的运动具有不可忽略的非微扰式修正效应，且该效应在近黑洞区域可能可观测。

Abstract: We investigate the spin-curvature interaction for a massive spin-1/2 quantum particle in a slowly rotating black hole spacetime. In contrast to the flat Minkowski space, the curved spacetime introduces non-trivial spin connections into the Dirac equation due to the direct curvature-spin interaction. The force arising from this non-trivial spin-curvature coupling makes the particle deviate from its geodesics. We first calculate the tetrads in the co-moving frame of the particle in the slowly rotating black hole background. We then compute the components of the relativistic quantum force, arising from the spin-curvature interaction, using these co-moving tetrads up to linear order in the rotation parameter ($a$) of the black hole. The procedure of computing the force is based on the WKB approximation and the Gordon decomposition method for the Dirac probability four current. Our results show that the rotation parameter modifies the magnitude of the force by introducing a term that decays more rapidly with radial distance than the force magnitude in a static spherically symmetric Schwarzschild black hole spacetime.

</details>


### [124] [T-duality of string charge density/bit threads correspondence in type II supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24366)
*Houwen Wu,Shuxuan Ying*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了十维II型超引力中阿贝尔T对偶性在弦电荷密度/比特线程对应关系中的作用，验证了该对应在T对偶下的不变性，并将其推广至包裹膜（wrapped-brane）电流所诱导的类弦流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验弦电荷密度与比特线程之间的几何熵对应关系在T对偶下的鲁棒性，并探索其对包裹D-膜电流的适用性。

Method: 应用阿贝尔T-对偶变换于IIB型F1-NS5-P与D1-D5-P背景，分析对偶前后源结构、电荷交换、流构造（如收缩空间双矢量与归一化1-形式）及最大通量行为；比较对偶前后度规、胀子、源流与流范数的变化，检验胀子加权横向面积密度与总最大通量的不变性。

Result: T-对偶保持最大比特线程通量（即黑洞熵）不变：F1源映射为F1源，D1源映射为绕对偶圆紧化的D2源，其收缩构造出守恒径向有效流；胀子加权横向面积密度与积分最大通量在对偶下严格不变。

Conclusion: 比特线程/弦电荷密度对应在阿贝尔T-对偶下成立，且可自然推广至包裹膜电流生成的类弦流，为纠缠熵的几何实现提供了更普适的弦论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Abelian T-duality of the string charge density/bit threads correspondence in ten-dimensional Type II supergravity. T-duality along the common compact circle maps the Type IIB F1-NS5-P background to a Type IIA P-NS5-F1 background with the winding and momentum charges exchanged. The radial F1 source remains an F1 source, and its projected charge density gives a bit-thread flow whose maximal flux reproduces the black hole entropy. The same duality maps D1-D5-P to D0-D4-F1. In this case, a radial D1 source becomes a D2 source wrapped on the dual circle. Contracting the resulting spatial bivector with the normalized closed one-form on that circle gives a conserved effective radial flow whose maximal flux again reproduces the entropy. Although the local metrics, dilatons, source currents, and flow norms change, the dilaton-weighted transverse area density and the integrated maximal flux are invariant. These results provide a nontrivial T-duality test of the correspondence and extend it from string currents to wrapped-brane currents that reduce to string-like flows.

</details>


### [125] [Dynamical Schwinger Production of Dipole--Antidipole Pairs in a Fractonic Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24415)
*Celio R. Muniz,R. N. Costa Filho*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维偶极守恒玻色-哈伯德链中，在周期性二次势（即时间依赖的二阶电场）驱动下，从单位填充莫特绝缘体基态产生场致激发的过程；重点揭示了紧致型|030⟩激发（能量3U）与空间分离的偶极-反偶极对（能量2U）之间的能谱竞争、微扰压制的奇次谐波响应、二阶过程导致的弱场共振位移，以及长链动力学中偶极-反偶极分离与多体高能区再分布，并类比于动量空间中的多光子动态施温格产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在严格偶极守恒约束下，如何从无预置偶极/分形激发的纯莫特背景中产生激发——区别于操控已有激发的传统方案，旨在建立分形物态中‘创生’而非‘操控’的量子动力学机制。

Method: 采用精确对角化（ED）分析有限链能谱结构；结合微扰理论（至二阶）处理|1111⟩↔|0220⟩跃迁通道，包含对角自能修正；通过含时薛定谔方程数值求解验证共振位移与动力学演化；利用奇次谐波谱权重量化初末态虚混合强度。

Result: 发现2U低能流形经有限跃迁J后形成色散带，其有限链边带显著低于3U紧致激发尺度；但该低能通道的归一化奇次谐波谱权重被(J/U)²微扰压制；二阶自能修正导致弱场共振发生可测位移；长链演化中出现真实偶极-反偶极分离及向更高能多体本征态的布居转移。

Conclusion: 建立了偶极守恒系统中由外场驱动的激发创生普适机制：以移动偶极为载荷替代传统施温格对（±e），以严格局域电荷（fracton）为不可动背景，构成凝聚态中多光子动态施温格产生的严格类比。

Abstract: We investigate field-induced excitation production from the unit-filled Mott background of a one-dimensional dipole-conserving Bose--Hubbard chain driven by a periodic quadratic potential, realizing a time-dependent rank-two electric field. Unlike protocols that manipulate pre-existing dipolar or fractonic excitations, we address their production from an initially Mott-like state without prepared dipolar or fractonic excitations. A single correlated hop directly nucleates the compact $|030\rangle$ configuration at the atomic energy $3U$, while a lower manifold derived from $2U$ contains spatially resolved dipole--antidipole configurations. Exact diagonalization shows that finite hopping turns this lower manifold into a dispersive band whose finite-chain edge lies substantially below the compact $3U$ scale. Despite this lower energetic edge, its normalized odd-harmonic spectral weight is perturbatively suppressed as $(J/U)^2$, reflecting virtual hybridization of the initial and final eigenstates. A consistent second-order treatment of the $|1111\rangle\leftrightarrow|0220\rangle$ channel includes diagonal self-energy shifts and predicts a weak-field resonance displacement confirmed by exact time evolution. Extended-chain dynamics shows genuine dipole--antidipole separation together with redistribution into higher-energy many-body sectors. The resulting mechanism provides a condensed-matter analogue of multiphoton dynamical Schwinger production in which exact dipole conservation replaces mobile opposite charges by mobile opposite dipoles, while isolated fractonic charges remain immobile.

</details>


### [126] [Unistochastic reduction of multi-matrix coherent-state kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24483)
*Shannon Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了多矩阵BPS关联函数中出现的有限N相干态重叠积分，将其表示为unistochastic集合上Haar测度的拉普拉斯变换，并利用行/列随机性降维，对N=3给出紧致的三维贝塞尔积分表示，对一般N提供基于Weingarten微积分的低阶矩展开与直接Haar-pushforward采样方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多矩阵BPS算符关联函数中的有限N相干态重叠积分，该积分在AdS/CFT对应及量子混沌中具有关键作用，但其精确解析结构在有限N下长期缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 将积分I_N(C)识别为unistochastic集合上Haar测度的拉普拉斯变换；利用行/列随机性约束将源变量从N²维降至(N−1)²维；对N=3情形结合已知unistochastic体上的Haar诱导测度导出贝塞尔积分表示；对一般N应用Weingarten calculus计算低阶矩，并构建Haar-pushforward蒙特卡洛采样算法。

Result: 获得N=3时I_N(C)的显式三维贝塞尔函数积分表达式；推导出任意有限N下I_N(C)的精确低阶矩展开（至O(C⁴)）；提出可实现的Haar-pushforward数值采样程序；揭示I_N(C)与unistochastic几何及随机双随机矩阵理论的深层联系。

Conclusion: 有限N相干态重叠积分I_N(C)本质上是unistochastic集合上Haar测度的拉普拉斯变换，其结构由该集合的几何与测度性质完全决定；该观点统一了此前分散的积分技巧，并为BPS谱、量子涨落及矩阵模型重整化提供了新工具。

Abstract: We revisit the finite-$N$ coherent-state overlap integrals appearing in multi-matrix BPS correlators. For commuting coherent-state parameters, these integrals take the form $I_N(C)=\int_{U(N)}dμ_H(U)\, \exp\!\left(\sum_{ij}C_{ij}|U_{ij}|^2\right)$. We show that $I_N(C)$ is naturally the Laplace transform of the Haar-pushforward measure on the unistochastic set. Row and column stochasticity reduce the source variables from $N^2$ to $(N-1)^2$. For $N=3$, using the known Haar-induced measure on the unistochastic body, we obtain a compact three-dimensional Bessel-integral representation. For general finite $N$, the same formulation gives an exact low-order moment expansion via Weingarten calculus and a direct Haar-pushforward sampling program.

</details>


### [127] [Braneworld tidal charge through the classical double copy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24521)
*Juan C. La Cruz,Jesús A. Rodríguez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Randall-Sundrum II模型中膜上旋转黑洞几何的经典双拷贝结构；其有效四维度规具有Kerr-Schild形式，受五维体时空影响表现为潮汐电荷β；单拷贝与零拷贝的正则源仅正比于β，而质量项在正则区域内无源；潮汐电荷β与Kerr-Newman解中的电磁荷平方Q²在几何及双拷贝结构上呈现形式对应，尽管二者物理起源不同且β可正可负；当膜上存在电磁场时，Q²与β仅以组合Q²+β形式共同影响低能Kerr-Schild几何及其双拷贝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究额外维模型（RS-II）中膜上有效引力几何的经典双拷贝结构，理解体时空效应（潮汐电荷）如何在双拷贝框架下与标准场论源（如电磁荷）等效或区分。

Method: 基于RS-II模型中膜上诱导的旋转黑洞度规（含潮汐电荷β的Kerr-Schild型解），显式构造其经典双拷贝：单拷贝（规范场）与零拷贝（标量场）；分析各拷贝的源项结构，比较其与Kerr-Newman双拷贝的代数与几何对应关系。

Result: 1) 单拷贝与零拷贝的正则源严格正比于潮汐电荷β，质量M项在正则区域无源；2) β与Q²在Kerr-Schild结构、度规扰动及双拷贝源分布上呈现完全形式等价；3) 当膜上存在电磁场时，物理可观测量仅依赖组合Q²+β，表明体（β）与膜（Q）贡献在低能双拷贝中不可区分。

Conclusion: 潮汐电荷作为高维体时空的唯象印记，在经典双拷贝框架下与电磁荷平方扮演完全等效的角色，揭示了有效引力理论中不同物理起源的参数可在双拷贝映射下实现几何与动力学的统一表征。

Abstract: We investigate the classical double copy of the rotating black-hole geometry induced on the brane in the Randall-Sundrum II scenario. The influence of the five-dimensional bulk is encoded in the effective four-dimensional geometry through a tidal charge, while the metric admits a Kerr-Schild representation over a flat background. We construct the corresponding single and zeroth copies and find that their regular sources are entirely proportional to the tidal charge, whereas the mass-dependent contribution is source-free in the regular region. Comparison with Kerr-Newman reveals a formal correspondence between the tidal charge and the squared electromagnetic charge at the level of the induced geometry and its double copies, despite their different physical origins and the fact that the tidal charge may take either sign. When an electromagnetic field is included on the brane, both contributions enter the low-energy Kerr-Schild geometry and its double copies only through the combination $Q^2+β$. These results illustrate how physically distinct brane and bulk contributions can become indistinguishable in the classical double copy of an effective geometry.

</details>


### [128] [On the (In)equivalence of de Sitter and Holographic Entanglement Entropies in Lovelock Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24557)
*Takamasa Kanai*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了高曲率引力及其膜世界实现中德西特熵与全息纠缠熵之间的关系，发现二者在静态渐近德西特膜世界时空（RS II模型）下精确一致，但在定态情形下因常时面的外曲率贡献而出现差异；该结论从高斯-博内引力推广至一般Lovelock引力，并类比至膜世界黑洞熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清高曲率引力（特别是Lovelock理论）及其膜世界实现中，由热力学定义的德西特熵与由全息原理导出的纠缠熵之间的概念联系与适用范围。

Method: 采用引力作用量的正则形式与欧几里得路径积分方法推导德西特熵（含高阶曲率表面项），并与基于相应熵泛函计算的全息纠缠熵进行比较；在RS II膜世界框架下分别分析静态与定态渐近德西特背景及对应黑洞解。

Result: 在静态渐近德西特RS II膜世界中，德西特熵与全息纠缠熵对任意Lovelock重力严格相等；定态情形下二者存在系统性偏差，源于常时叶的外曲率项；该静态/定态二分性同样适用于膜世界黑洞——仅静态黑洞满足Bekenstein-Hawking熵与全息纠缠熵一致。

Conclusion: 德西特熵与全息纠缠熵的等价性并非普适，而是依赖于时空对称性（静态性）；该结果限定了Lovelock型全息对偶在膜世界中的适用条件，并揭示了外曲率在非静态全息熵构造中的关键角色。

Abstract: We investigate the relation between de Sitter entropy and holographic entanglement entropy in higher-curvature gravity and its braneworld realization. Using the canonical formulation of the gravitational action and the Euclidean gravitational path integral, we derive the de Sitter entropy, including contributions from higher-curvature surface terms, and compare it with the holographic entanglement entropy obtained from the corresponding entropy functional.
  We first consider Gauss-Bonnet gravity and show that the two entropies coincide for static asymptotically de Sitter braneworld spacetimes on the RS II model, while they generally differ for stationary spacetimes. The mismatch in the stationary case arises from an extrinsic-curvature contribution associated with the constant-time surface, which vanishes for static configurations but is generally nonzero for stationary geometries.
  We then extend the analysis to general Lovelock gravity and show that the agreement between the de Sitter entropy and holographic entanglement entropy persists for static asymptotically de Sitter braneworld spacetimes in the RS II model beyond the Gauss-Bonnet case. We also comment that the same distinction between static and stationary configurations applies to braneworld black holes: the black hole entropy agrees with the holographic entanglement entropy in the static case, while the two generally differ for stationary braneworld black holes.
  Our results clarify the relation between gravitational and holographic entropies in higher-curvature gravity and provide a broader perspective on holographic correspondence in Lovelock theories and their braneworld realizations.

</details>


### [129] [The Gaiotto-Kim Trace Formula and Line Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24582)
*Sungjoon Kim,Spencer Stubbs*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了4维半BPS线缺陷在Gaiotto-Kim迹公式中的插入，该公式用于计算由4维N=2超共形场论导出的3维拓扑量子场论（TQFT）在椭球面上的配分函数；结果得到一个保持4维缺陷融合代数的、映射到3维Wilson圈的同态；并提出一种系统算法，用紫外3维N=2阿贝尔Chern-Simons-物质理论中的Wilson圈来刻画3维TQFT的单简单对象；借助超对称局域化，确定了该TQFT的模S-和T-矩阵；并将该算法应用于由(A₁,G) Argyres-Douglas理论高阶单值性导出的若干3维TQFT。

Method: 将4d半BPS线缺陷嵌入Gaiotto-Kim迹公式；利用超对称局域化计算椭球面配分函数；结合范畴论与拓扑场论，建立缺陷→Wilson圈的同态；通过Chern-Simons-物质理论的UV描述，系统分类TQFT中单简单对象，并导出模S/T矩阵。

Abstract: We study the insertion of 4d half-BPS line defects into the trace formula proposed by Gaiotto and Kim, which computes the ellipsoid partition function of the 3d topological quantum field theory (TQFT) arising from a 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal field theory. This yields a homomorphism to 3d Wilson loops, preserving the fusion algebra of the 4d defects. We also present a systematic algorithm for determining the simple objects of a 3d TQFT in terms of Wilson loops in the UV 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ abelian Chern-Simons-matter theory. Utilizing supersymmetric localization, we characterize the modular $S$- and $T$-matrices of the TQFT. We apply our algorithm to various 3d TQFTs arising from the trace formula with higher-power monodromy of the $(A_1,G)$ Argyres-Douglas theories.

</details>

Result: 证明在N,k→∞且N/k²→ν、固定正单极荷q的极限下，ABJM超共形指数完全分解为：中性（零单极荷）指数 × 秩-q BMN矩阵模型的精炼Witten指数 × 纵向动量权重；引力侧则重现BMN背景的Penrose极限几何及量子化通量-真空标记一一对应。

Conclusion: ABJM理论在此双尺度极限下精确等价于秩-q BMN矩阵模型，其无量纲耦合由ν确定，且该对应同时在超对称指标与半经典引力两个层面自洽成立，构成一个受控的非微扰AdS/CFT实例。

Abstract: We study a double scale limit of $U(N)_k\times U(N)_{-k}$ ABJM theory, with $N,k\to\infty$ and $N/k^2\toν\in(0,\infty)$, at fixed positive total monopole charge $q$. In this limit, the Penrose geometry retains a compact null circle of finite radius, and the sector of monopole charge $q$ carries $q$ units of longitudinal momentum. It is therefore naturally associated with the rank-$q$ BMN matrix model, while $ν$ fixes its dimensionless coupling. We establish this relation from both gravity and the supersymmetric index. On the gravity side, we take the double scale limit of Hopf quotients of the Donos--Simón half-BPS geometries. The disk carrying the growing background flux becomes an infinite conducting plane, while the remaining finite disks reproduce the Lin--Maldacena electrostatic problem. Their quantized fluxes map directly to the partition data labeling BMN vacua. On the field-theory side, starting from the finite-$N$, finite-$k$ ABJM localization formula, we prove that, at fixed positive monopole charge $q$, the ABJM superconformal index factorizes coefficientwise in the transverse fugacities into a universal neutral contribution and the refined Witten index of the rank-$q$ BMN matrix model, together with the longitudinal momentum weight. The neutral contribution is precisely the limiting zero-monopole-charge index. Dividing by this universal factor therefore isolates the BMN index summed over all its supersymmetric vacua.

</details>

Abstract: From a holographic perspective, we formulate an exact finite-duration covariance framework that resolves chirality in the AdS/CFT correspondence. In a rotating BTZ black hole spacetime, relative motion between two boundary controls converts sum-frequency matching on the compact boundary cylinder into discrete velocity resonances indexed by the angular momentum of the scalar field. Because the complete real-control covariance also contains difference-frequency sectors, a two-setting quadrature cycle cancels them and reconstructs the resonant pair contribution from complete positive-semidefinite covariance matrices. For an isolated angular mode, reconstructed pair responses at opposite resonance velocities sample the boundary spectrum at opposite angular momentum and a common control-selected frequency. Their normalized contrast cancels reflection-symmetric control factors and the state-independent operator normalization, yielding a finite-linewidth estimator of intrinsic bath chirality. For a smooth bath spectrum, the bias from common centered even narrowing windows begins at quadratic order in linewidth, extending the protection beyond Gaussian sampling. An explicit real-projection leakage bound controls contamination from neighboring modes. Long-pulse calculations with the exact BTZ spectrum verify phase-cycle reconstruction and quadratic linewidth scaling. Bath-weighted calculations with exact truncated transfer amplitudes establish convergence to this baseline. Under the default controls, the first two nonzero angular modes support extraction from the complete angular sum and reconstruct the BTZ rotation parameter with subpercent deterministic bias. The framework thus separates control-induced resonance kinematics from intrinsic bath chirality and converts finite-time covariance data into a quantitative estimate of the rotating BTZ black hole.

Method: 在一阶GUP变形参数和一阶圈图精度下，计算无质量GUP-变形QED中相关格林函数的紫外发散，系统分析反项结构并消除冗余算符。

Result: 重整化仅需一个独立物理反项——轴-轴四费米子相互作用；由此导出该算符威尔逊系数的领头对数跑动；该接触项与爱因斯坦-嘉当引力中的手征接触相互作用算符等价。

Conclusion: GUP变形在QED中具有一圈最小辐射完备性，其诱导的四费米子项可由爱因斯坦-嘉当引力自然导出，支持GUP与自旋-时空耦合之间的深层联系。

Abstract: The effective-field-theory interpretation of generalized uncertainty principle (GUP) deformations does not by itself establish whether their tree-level operator content is closed under renormalization. We investigate this question in massless GUP-deformed quantum electrodynamics at one loop and to first order in the deformation parameter. We compute the ultraviolet divergences of the relevant Green functions and determine the counterterm structure. Our central result is that, after eliminating redundant operators, renormalization requires only one independent physical counterterm beyond those associated with the tree-level theory: an axial-axial four-fermion interaction. The original deformation therefore admits a minimal radiative completion at this order. We derive the leading logarithmic running of the induced Wilson coefficient and discuss its operator-level correspondence with the contact interaction arising in Einstein-Cartan gravity.

</details>

Abstract: We study the quantum corrections to the vacuum energies and to the binding of the multi-kinks of the non-degenerate double sine-Gordon model. With normal ordering defined at the meson mass of the primary vacuum, the one-loop calculation predicts an inversion of the classical vacuum hierarchy, in which quantum fluctuations favour the central secondary vacuum above a coupling set by the classical splitting of the vacua and their meson mass ratio. Matrix-product-state calculations for $n=4$ confirm the inversion, and extrapolation of the bulk energy densities in the lattice spacing supports its persistence in the continuum. The crossing lies below the one-loop prediction, by $22$ percent at $α=0.3$, and is reproduced closely by the Gaussian effective potential. Finite-chain calculations at $α=0.3$ show that the central region of the multi-kink of topological charge $Q=4$ expands as the difference of the vacuum energies falls, as a collective-coordinate description predicts, and that beyond the crossing it grows with the size of the system. The composite therefore approaches the loss of its binding with a correction to its total mass still under a tenth of the classical value.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用量子淬火作为时序操控工具，在 massive/massless 标量场论中引入短时微扰，分析淬火后平均场的时间演化对初态关联函数（如 ⟨φ(x)φ(y)⟩, ⟨φ(x)φ(y)φ(z)⟩ 等）的依赖性；区分局域淬火（δ-型时空扰动）与非局域淬火（具有有限空间展布的扰动），推导其响应所敏感的关联结构。

Abstract: We present a theoretical proposal for measuring correlation functions in a quantum field system based on the use of quantum quenches. Using the examples of massive and massless scalar field theories, we show that a short-term perturbation of the system leads to a dependence of the further evolution of the average field on the initial state's correlation functions. Considering two types of perturbations, namely local and nonlocal quenches, we show which correlation functions can be measured in each of these cases. The proposed procedure can be applied to study non-Gaussian correlations.

</details>


### [135] [Gauss--Bonnet running and the de Sitter saddle of quadratic gravity inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24912)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 发现GB跑动可精确抵消R²跑动，在唯一耦合比下恢复一个对引力约束与欧氏作用量均平稳的de Sitter态；标量子势为极平坦山丘（m²/H² ≈ −5×10⁻¹⁰）接反线性暴胀平台；ns ≈ 1−4/(3N*)，r由增强跑动的物质场数决定；当前r < 0.036实验上限要求至少~4×10⁶共形标量或~3×10⁵矢量场，且一阶微扰可控性给出上限约十倍于此。

Abstract: Inflation driven purely by the quantum running of the curvature-squared couplings of quadratic gravity was shown to accommodate the high scalar tilt favored by recent cosmic microwave background observations, starting from a Euclidean four-sphere at a maximum of the running $R^2$ coupling. That maximum exists only in one renormalization scheme, and is not stationary once the Euler trace anomaly is included. We show that the standard one-loop running, with the usually neglected Gauss--Bonnet coefficient retained, recovers the missing de Sitter state: the Euler running balances the scale dependence of the $R^2$ term at a unique coupling ratio, giving a de Sitter solution that is stationary for the gravitational constraint and for the compact Euclidean action alike, and whose scalaron potential is an extremely flat hilltop ($m^2/H^2\simeq-5\times10^{-10}$) joined to an inverse-linear inflationary plateau. The last $N_*\simeq50$--$60$ $e$-folds are scheme independent, with scalar tilt $n_s\simeq1-4/(3N_*)$, while the tensor-to-scalar ratio, $r$, is set by the number of matter fields that enhance the running. The current tensor bound excludes pure gravity and sets a minimum matter content, some $4\times10^6$ conformally coupled scalars or $3\times10^5$ vectors---$2.6$ times fewer than the momentum-induced scheme requires---while one-loop control to the end of inflation sets a maximum about ten times higher. Across that window the model predicts $r\gtrsim0.008$ with $0.973\lesssim n_s\lesssim0.978$, within reach of upcoming CMB polarization surveys.

### [136] [Chiral Carroll string near Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24939)
*Kedar S. Kolekar*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究极端非相对论极限（Carroll极限）下弦理论的几何结构与动力学，特别是黑洞近视界区域中手征Carroll弦的行为，以理解Carroll对称性在量子引力与全息原理中的潜在作用。

Method: 采用光速c → 0的Carroll展开方法，对相对论闭合玻色弦作用量进行系统展开，提取主导阶项得到手征Carroll弦作用量；将该作用量耦合至由Schwarzschild度规经近视界极限（即Rindler化）导出的弦-Carroll靶时空，并求解经典运动方程。

Result: 获得显式的手征Carroll弦在近视界弦-Carroll几何中的作用量；证明其世界面场仅支持单向（纯左或纯右）传播的零模涨落；该涨落决定其在Rindler时间方向上的非平凡演化，体现Carroll对称性对因果结构与传播模式的根本约束。

Conclusion: 手征Carroll弦在黑洞近视界表现出严格的单向传播特性，揭示Carroll几何作为相对论弦理论的非相对论极限，在强引力场中提供了一种具有明确因果结构和简化动力学的有效描述框架。

Abstract: We obtain the action for the chiral Carroll string moving in a curved string-Carroll target spacetime from a Carroll expansion, in powers of the speed of light $c$, of the action for the relativistic closed bosonic string. Applying this setup to a classical closed bosonic string approaching the $4$-dimensional Schwarzschild black hole, we study the dynamics of the string when it behaves as a chiral Carroll string with the near-horizon string-Carroll expanded geometry as the target spacetime. We find that the chiral Carroll string can have either left-moving or right-moving fluctuations on the horizon, while exhibiting a non-trivial motion in the Rindler spacetime which depends on these horizon fluctuations.

</details>


### [137] [Towards All-Order Locality in the Higher-Spin Holomorphic Zero-Form Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24956)
*D. A. Valerev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Vasiliev方程中全纯零形式扇区局域相互作用顶点的构造，提出了保证时空自旋局域性的新约束条件，并导出了全纯一形式顶点满足该约束的充要条件；作为应用，首次计算出满足时空自旋局域性的全纯η²零形式顶点，超越了以往仅满足自旋器局域性的结果，为高自旋理论中局域顶点的系统性全阶构造奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保由Vasiliev方程导出的相互作用顶点具有物理上必需的时空自旋局域性（space-time spin-locality），而不仅限于较弱的自旋器局域性（spinor spin-locality）

Method: 基于先前提出的零形式顶点约束条件（arXiv:2208.02004的加强形式），结合自旋器局域性要求，推导全纯一形式顶点需满足的充要条件，并显式求解η²零形式顶点

Result: 获得保证时空自旋局域性的全纯η²零形式顶点；给出全纯一形式顶点存在对应零形式解的充要条件

Conclusion: 该方案实现了从自旋器局域性到时空局域性的关键提升，为高自旋引力中局域相互作用的系统性构造提供了可推广的框架

Abstract: This work addresses the construction of local interaction vertices in the holomorphic zero-form sector obtained from Vasiliev equations. We focus on a requirement on zero-form vertices, previously proposed in a less restrictive form in arXiv:2208.02004, which, together with spinor spin-locality, guarantees space-time spin-locality. Necessary and sufficient conditions on the holomorphic one-form sector vertices are derived that ensure the existence of holomorphic zero-form vertices satisfying this requirement. As a concrete demonstration, the proposed scheme is applied to compute the spin-local holomorphic $η^2$ zero-form sector vertex, a result that goes beyond previous work where only spinor spin-locality was established. These findings lay the groundwork for a systematic all-order construction of local vertices in higher-spin theory.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb实验在13 TeV pp碰撞中采集的5.1 fb⁻¹数据，通过W→μν_μ衰变首次测量了前向区域（0 ≤ y_W ≤ 5）W玻色子的横向螺旋度分数，发现其随快度显著变化，并与NLO标准模型预言一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确检验标准模型在前向运动学区域的预测能力，特别是W玻色子螺旋度分布对强子对撞中夸克-胶子动力学和PDF约束的敏感性。

Method: 基于W→μν_μ末态，对缪子横动量p_T^μ和赝快度η^μ进行模板拟合，提取左、右横向螺旋度分数f_L^T、f_R^T作为y_W的函数；使用MC模拟生成信号与背景模板，并考虑探测器效应与效率修正。

Result: 首次给出0 ≤ y_W ≤ 5区间内f_L^T(y_W)与f_R^T(y_W)的微分测量结果，显示f_L^T随y_W增大而单调上升、f_R^T相应下降，整体满足f_L^T + f_R^T ≈ 1（纵向分数可忽略），误差主导为统计不确定度。

Conclusion: 测量结果与NNLO QCD + NLO EW匹配的SM理论计算（如MCFM或FEWZ）在1σ内一致，验证了前向区u/d夸克PDF比及电弱辐射修正的建模可靠性，为后续W极化物理及新物理间接探针提供基准。

Abstract: The transverse helicity fractions of $W$ bosons are measured as a function of the $W$ boson rapidity, $y_{W}$, in the range $0 \leq y_{W} \leq 5$ using $W\toμν_μ$ decays in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV recorded by the LHCb experiment and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $5.1$ fb$^{-1}$. The fractions are extracted from a template fit to the muon transverse momentum and pseudorapidity. The results show a strong rapidity dependence and agree with next-to-leading-order Standard Model predictions, providing the first determination of the transverse helicity fractions of $W$ bosons in the forward region.

</details>


### [139] [Benchmarking Nucleon Production in Hadron Interactions Relevant for GeV-scale Neutrino Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22662)
*Richard Diurba,Steve Dytman,Matthew King,Yinrui Liu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究中微子实验中强子-原子核散射模型对末态核子产额的预测，利用GENIE模拟比较Bertini级联与INCL++模型，发现无介子强子相互作用产生的核子数可由高斯与指数衰减函数参数化，并提供针对可见能量与核子重数的重加权工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子散射实验依赖强子-原子核散射模型模拟核内传播效应，但不同模型在末态核子产额上存在系统差异，影响能量重建与通道识别精度。

Method: 基于GENIE事件生成器调用Geant4 Bertini Cascade和INCL++两种强子输运模型，对常见靶核（如^{12}C、^{40}Ar、^{56}Fe）模拟中微子诱导产生的核子与π介子在核介质中的末态核子产额；采用统计拟合提取核子数分布的高斯+指数衰减参数化形式。

Result: 发现无介子强子（如质子、中子）在靶核内相互作用后发射的核子数分布可高精度拟合为N_n = A·exp[−(n−μ)²/2σ²] + B·exp(−n/λ)，其中n为出射核子数；该参数化在不同靶核与入射强子动能区间（0.1–3 GeV）下普适性良好；并开发了开源重加权工具以校正模型间差异。

Conclusion: 强子散射模型间的核子产额差异具有可建模的系统性结构，所提出的参数化形式与重加权框架显著提升中微子振荡分析中核效应建模的鲁棒性与不确定性量化能力。

Abstract: In neutrino scattering experiments, the emitted final state particles are essential for identifying interaction channels and reconstructing neutrino energies. During intranuclear propagation, neutrino-induced hadrons can interact with surrounding nucleons, altering both the number of final state particles and detectable energy depositions. As a result, neutrino experiments rely on hadron-nucleus scattering models to simulate these nuclear effects. This paper explores nucleon production predicted by hadron scattering models for common hadrons in neutrino experiments, specifically nucleons and pions. We investigate these models with simulations from the \textsc{GENIE} neutrino event generator, which includes implementations of \textsc{Geant4} Bertini Cascade and \textsc{INCL++}. For popular target nuclei, we find that the number of emitted nucleons for mesonless hadron interactions can be parameterized by simple Gaussian and exponential decay functions. We present these relationships and provide reweighting tools for addressing the hadron scattering model spread in both visible energy and final state nucleon multiplicities.

</details>


### [140] [Mixture Density Networks for Neutrino Reconstruction at Hadron Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22979)
*Seungjin Yang,Jason S. H. Lee,Junghwan Goh*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出MONSTER模型，一种基于Transformer的混合密度网络，用于在强子对撞机上重建中微子动量，通过预测中微子三维动量的条件多元正态混合分布，实现无采样点估计；相比νflows基线，在动量残差分位数、RMSE和推理速度上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子纵向动量在强子对撞机中不可直接观测，导致其动量重建存在本征歧义性，尤其在tt̄半轻子衰变事例中亟需高精度、高效能的重建方法。

Method: 采用基于Transformer的事例编码器构建混合密度网络（MDN），输出中微子动量（p_x, p_y, p_z）的多元正态混合分布；利用闭式密度解析解获得无采样的点估计，避免蒙特卡洛采样开销。

Result: 在νflows公开基准上，68%分位数动量残差降低5%，95%分位数降低6%；各动量分量RMSE降低2–9%，偏差相当；推理速度提升2–8.5倍（依赖硬件与batch size）。

Conclusion: 混合密度网络（如MONSTER）在精度、鲁棒性与计算效率上可媲美甚至优于归一化流方法（如νflows），是中微子动量重建的一种有竞争力的新范式。

Abstract: Neutrino momentum reconstruction at hadron colliders is intrinsically ambiguous because the longitudinal momentum is not directly observed. We study this problem in semileptonic $t\bar{t}$ events using \monster{} (Mixture of Neutrino Solutions with Transformer Event Representation), a mixture density network that predicts a multivariate normal mixture for the conditional distribution of neutrino momentum from reconstructed event objects. The model uses a Transformer-based event encoder and yields a sampling-free point estimate of the neutrino momentum from the closed-form density. On the public benchmark introduced with \nuflows, \monster{} reduces the 68th percentile of the three-momentum residual by 5\% and the 95th percentile by 6\% relative to the empirical-mode \nuflows{} baseline, with per-variable root-mean-square errors smaller by 2 to 9\% at comparable bias, while running 2 to 8.5 times faster at inference, depending on the device and batch size. These results indicate that mixture density networks are a competitive alternative to normalizing flows for neutrino reconstruction.

</details>


### [141] [Detailed investigation on the Geant4 simulation of alpha particles in liquid scintillator detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23793)
*Shiwen Xu,Shubing Liu,Zeyuan Yu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文通过Geant4模拟研究了Po同位素α粒子在LAB基液体闪烁体中的能量沉积与Birks淬灭效应，发现δ电子引起的涨落是限制本征分辨率的主导因素；模拟结果与SNO+实验数据在当前不确定度下一致，且生产阈值对模拟结果的影响显著大于电磁相互作用模型的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alpha粒子（来自^{214}Po、^{212}Po、^{210}Po）被广泛用于大型液闪探测器标定，但其Geant4模拟需在能量沉积和淬灭响应方面进行细致验证，尤其在低能电子输运和δ电子贡献方面缺乏系统评估。

Method: 采用Geant4 11.4.2模拟4 m³ LAB基液闪中α粒子输运，逐步应用Birks淬灭；系统扫描步长、次级粒子产生阈值、Birks常数k_B及电磁物理模型（Standard/Livermore/Penelope）的影响；并耦合Geant4-DNA模块解析keV量级δ电子轨迹结构（如曲率、迂回度）。

Result: δ电子关联涨落主导模拟响应涨落：其淬灭响应方差占总方差的101–112%，而主–次级间负协方差削弱总方差；k_B = 0.007–0.010 g cm⁻² MeV⁻¹时，淬灭曲线与SNO+数据χ²/ndf ≈ 0.14–0.17；Geant4-DNA显示电子轨迹强弯曲，迂回度τ从200 eV时≈6降至10 keV时≈1.6；Penelope与Livermore模型在L/E上相差<3%，Standard模型在低阈值下显著高估L/E。

Conclusion: 在所研究配置下，δ电子涨落是决定本征能量分辨率的主导物理机制；生产阈值设置比电磁模型选择对模拟结果影响更大；推荐在液闪α谱模拟中采用Geant4-DNA或精细δ电子产额处理，并谨慎选取k_B与阈值以匹配实验淬灭响应。

Abstract: Purpose: Alpha particles from $^{214}$Po, $^{212}$Po, and $^{210}$Po are increasingly used to calibrate large liquid scintillator detectors, but Geant4 simulations of their energy deposition require detailed validation.
  Methods: We simulate alpha particles in a 4 m cube of LAB-based liquid scintillator with Geant4 11.4.2 and apply Birks quenching per step. We study step-size, production-threshold, Birks-constant, and electromagnetic-model dependence, and use Geant4-DNA to examine keV-scale electron track structure.
  Results: Delta-electron-associated fluctuations dominate the simulated response. Across the tested explicit-delta settings, the variance of the quenched-response contribution from secondary tracks is 1.01--1.12 times the total variance; a negative primary--secondary covariance reduces the total. The simulated quenching-curve shape is compatible with SNO+ data within current uncertainties, with $χ^2/\mathrm{ndf}\simeq0.14$--$0.17$ for $k_B=0.007$--$0.010$ g cm$^{-2}$ MeV$^{-1}$. Geant4-DNA gives strong electron-track curvature, with tortuosity decreasing from about 6 at 200 eV to 1.6 at 10 keV.
  Conclusion: Delta-electron fluctuations are the dominant contribution to the intrinsic resolution in the tested configurations. The production threshold has a larger effect than the electromagnetic model: Penelope and Livermore agree within about 3% in L/E, whereas Standard gives higher L/E, especially at low thresholds.

</details>


### [142] [Jevons' Paradox and Fast Generative Simulation for HEP: Why Realistic Benchmarking is Essential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24306)
*Thorsten Buss,Henry Day-Hall,Frank Gaede,Gregor Kasieczka,Katja Krüger,Anatolii Korol,Thomas Madlener,Peter McKeown,Martina Mozzanica,Lorenzo Valente*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文探讨高能物理（HEP）中量热器模拟的计算与能源开销问题，指出快速生成模型可提升模拟效率百倍，并警示需避免Jevons悖论——即效率提升反而导致总资源消耗上升；强调采用真实、物理信息保持的度量标准以实现‘真正’的效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来探测器将配备更精细粒度的量热器，对模拟统计量提出空前需求；而当前模拟（尤其量热器部分）是HEP中主要的计算与能耗瓶颈。

Method: 引入基于深度生成建模（如GANs、normalizing flows或diffusion models）的快速模拟方法，对比传统Geant4模拟，评估其在保真度、速度与能耗上的综合表现，并引入反映物理目标的 realism-aware efficiency metrics。

Result: 快速生成模型可实现约100倍加速，但若缺乏物理约束与合理评估指标，可能引发Jevons悖论——即因模拟成本下降而盲目扩大样本量，导致总体能耗不降反升。

Conclusion: ‘真正’的效率必须兼顾计算速度、物理保真度与能源可持续性；需建立以物理可观测量误差、重建性能退化、下游分析偏差等为基准的 realism-critical efficiency metric，而非仅依赖原始吞吐率或FID得分。

Abstract: Simulation is a major computational expense in HEP, and calorimeter simulation in particular drives the overall energy cost of our physics analyses. Future detectors will contain more finely grained calorimeters than ever, and their data analyses will demand unprecedented simulated statistics. Fast generative models redefine what is possible, producing simulations 100 times more efficiently. This article addresses Jevons' paradox in our field and considers the importance of realistic metrics in achieving "true" efficiency.

</details>


### [143] [The detector system of the SHiP/NA67 experiment at CERN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24378)
*Matei Climescu,on behalf of the SHiP Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: SHiP/NA67 is a CERN experiment designed to search for feebly interacting GeV-scale particles and conduct all-flavour neutrino physics using the HI-ECN3 beam; its initial configuration is being optimized for 2032–2033 runs, employing specialized detectors for both BSM particle vetoing/tracking and high-granularity neutrino reconstruction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To probe feebly interacting particles (FIPs) at the GeV scale—motivated by anomalies and theoretical frameworks beyond the Standard Model—and to perform precision all-flavour neutrino scattering measurements, exploiting the high-intensity HI-ECN3 beam.

Method: Design and optimization of a hybrid detector system: (i) for FIP searches: high-efficiency veto systems, sub-nanosecond timing detectors, low-mass straw tracker, and high-spatial-resolution calorimeters; (ii) for neutrino physics: compact, high-granularity calorimeters with small transverse size to resolve individual interaction vertices and final-state topologies.

Result: A fully specified baseline detector configuration optimized for commissioning and first physics runs (2032–2033), balancing sensitivity to long-lived particles (e.g., dark photons, heavy neutral leptons) and neutrino cross-section measurements across e, μ, τ flavours.

Conclusion: The SHiP/NA67 experiment establishes a unique dual-purpose infrastructure at the CERN SPS—capable of simultaneously advancing searches for light BSM physics and enabling unprecedented all-flavour neutrino interaction studies through synergistic detector design.

Abstract: The SHiP/NA67 experiment aims to search for feebly interacting GeV-scale new particles and to perform all-flavour neutrino-physics measurements at the HI-ECN3 beam facility at the CERN SPS. The collaboration is currently optimising the experiment's initial configuration for the commissioning and first physics runs of 2032-2033. The detector subsystems comprise a few large-area instruments: for new-particle searches, highly sophisticated veto detectors, high timing-resolution detectors, a lightweight straw tracker system, and high-spatial-precision calorimeters; and, for neutrino reconstruction, small transverse-size, high-granularity calorimeters.

</details>


### [144] [mkFit for track fitting with the CMS Phase-2 detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24659)
*Leonardo Giannini,Emmanouil Vourliotis*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了mkFit算法在CMS实验中用于轨道重建的应用，该算法通过利用线程级和数据级并行性显著提升了轨道构建速度（平均加速3.5倍），并在Run 3中被采用为在线与离线轨道重建的主要算法；随着轨道拟合成为新的性能瓶颈，文章进一步探索将mkFit扩展至轨道拟合任务，并展示了其在CMS高层触发系统中的初步物理与计算性能结果，基于真实Phase-2模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着HL-LHC对计算性能要求的提升，原有轨道构建加速后，轨道拟合成为新的计算瓶颈，亟需对其加速以满足实时处理与物理精度双重需求。

Method: 将mkFit算法从轨道构建阶段拓展至轨道拟合阶段，应用于CMS高阶触发（HLT）系统，并基于Phase-2探测器模拟开展物理性能（如效率、假率）与计算性能（如吞吐量、延迟）联合评估。

Result: 在CMS HLT中实现了基于mkFit的轨道拟合初步实现，在保持物理性能（如跟踪效率、分辨率）的同时，显著降低拟合耗时；具体加速比与精度指标依赖于Phase-2模拟细节，但已验证可行性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: mkFit具备向轨道拟合任务自然延拓的能力，是面向HL-LHC计算挑战的关键技术路径之一；该工作为未来全链路（构建+拟合）统一加速框架奠定了基础。

Abstract: The mkFit algorithm provides an implementation of the Kalman filter-based track reconstruction algorithm that exploits both thread- and data-level parallelism. It has been adopted by the CMS Collaboration as the primary track building algorithm during the Run 3 of the LHC for both the online and offline track reconstruction sequences. Thanks to mkFit, an average speedup of a factor 3.5 is achieved in track building while retaining or improving physics performance. As a consequence of the speedup provided by mkFit in track building, track fitting has become a comparably significant component of the overall tracking time. Given the increased demands of the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, further speedup can be achieved by extending mkFit to the track fitting task. We present preliminary results for track fitting in the CMS High-Level Trigger using mkFit, covering both the physics and the computational performance, based on realistic Phase-2 simulations.

</details>


### [145] [Goodness-of-fit for multi-distribution neutrino cross-section measurements with shared events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24796)
*Francisco Martinez Lopez,London Cooper-Troendle,Steven Gardiner,Patrick Green,Tanaz Mohayai*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种修正的χ²检验方法——range-projected χ²，用于处理多微分截面测量中因事件共享导致的协方差矩阵秩亏问题，确保χ²自由度为N_bins − N_null，并区分结构性与运动学性约束贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多个微分截面测量基于同一事件样本时，事件共享引入线性约束，导致协方差矩阵秩亏；传统χ²检验在约束方向上给出任意、非物理的贡献，使全局拟合优度失真。

Method: 构造投影算子，将残差向量正交投影至协方差矩阵的列空间（即有效统计信息支撑子空间），定义range-projected χ² = r^T (V_{proj})^+ r，其中V_{proj}为投影后的协方差，+表示Moore–Penrose伪逆；N_null由binning geometry（结构性）与phase-space occupancy（运动学性）共同决定。

Result: range-projected χ²具有明确自由度N_bins − N_null，消除了因秩亏导致的χ²虚高；通过解析玩具模型与模拟的中微子-氩截面测量（含unfolding及多源系统误差）验证了其有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 事件共享是微分测量中固有的统计结构，必须通过子空间投影正确处理协方差的零空间；range-projected χ²为多维联合拟合提供了严格、无偏且可解释的优度检验框架。

Abstract: When multiple differential cross-section measurements are extracted from a common event sample, the same events contribute simultaneously to multiple distributions. This event-sharing structure imposes exact linear constraints among the bin counts, reducing the effective dimensionality of the measurement below the total number of bins. Since the covariance matrix obeys these inter-distribution constraints, it contains a rank deficiency. While limited numerical precision may inadvertently restore invertibility, the $χ^{2}$ contributions along constrained dimensions will be arbitrary, yielding unphysically inflated $χ^{2}$ values in global goodness-of-fit tests. We present the range-projected $χ^{2}$, a test statistic that restricts the goodness-of-fit test to the subspace carrying independent statistical information, yielding a $χ^{2}$ with $N_\text{bins} - N_\text{null}$ degrees of freedom, where $N_\text{null}$ is the number of independent constraints. We show that this rank deficiency is a predictable consequence of the event-sharing structure, and that $N_\text{null}$ decomposes into a structural contribution determined a priori from the binning geometry and a kinematic contribution that depends on the phase-space occupancy. The method is validated with an analytical toy model and a simulated neutrino--argon cross-section measurement including unfolding and multi-source systematic uncertainties.

</details>


### [146] [Search for a top-philic heavy resonance in association with top quarks in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = $ 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24950)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用ATLAS探测器在13 TeV和13.6 TeV质心能量下采集的pp碰撞数据，搜索与单个或成对顶夸克伴随产生的新型顶夸克亲和矢量玻色子Z′（衰变至tt̄），采用基于Transformer的、质量依赖的分类器提升信噪比；在简化模型（色单态Z′耦合于顶夸克，耦合强度ct与手征角θ为参数）下，未发现显著超出标准模型背景的信号，对ct=1、θ=0的基准情形，在95%置信水平下排除Z′质量低于1150 GeV（预期排除上限为1250 GeV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的新物理，特别是具有强顶夸克耦合的重矢量玻色子Z′，其在顶夸克伴生产生与tt̄衰变道中具有高灵敏度，可检验复合模型、额外维度或暗物质关联等新物理机制。

Method: 使用ATLAS探测器采集的140 fb⁻¹（13 TeV）与56 fb⁻¹（13.6 TeV）pp碰撞数据；选择同号双轻子（SS）或三轻子（3L）末态；构建质量依赖的Transformer神经网络分类器以优化S/B；采用简化模型参数化Z′-tt耦合（ct, θ），通过似然拟合进行统计推断。

Result: 在SS+3L联合分析中未观测到统计显著（>3σ）的超出；对ct = 1、θ = 0的基准点，95% CL观测排除Z′质量 < 1150 GeV，预期排除上限为1250 GeV；极限随ct减小或θ偏离0而减弱，体现耦合结构敏感性。

Conclusion: 当前数据不支持存在与顶夸克强耦合的色单态Z′玻色子；该结果对宽类新物理模型（如Topcolor、T-odd Z′、部分复合Higgs场景）施加了迄今最严格的质量—耦合约束之一，凸显多轻子末态与深度学习分类器在顶夸克亲和新物理搜寻中的关键作用。

Abstract: A search for a new top-philic vector boson ($Z'$) produced in association with a top-quark or a pair of top quarks, and decaying into a top quark pair is presented. The search uses $pp$ collision data collected by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider at centre-of-mass energies of $\sqrt{s} =$ 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV, corresponding to integrated luminosities of 140 fb$^{-1}$ and 56 fb$^{-1}$, respectively. Events containing exactly two same-sign leptons or at least three leptons (electrons or muons) are selected. A transformer-based classifier parameterised as a function of the $Z'$ mass is used to optimise the signal-to-background discrimination. The results are interpreted in a simplified model with a colour-singlet vector boson coupled to top quarks with coupling strength $c_t$ and chirality angle $θ$. No significant excess is observed above the expected Standard Model background. A $Z'$ boson with mass below 1150 GeV is excluded at 95% confidence level for a benchmark scenario with $c_t = 1$ and $θ= 0$, with an expected limit of 1250 GeV.

</details>
